# File: src/projectile.rs
//! CPU sphere proxies. Scratch storage is retained between frames.
use glam::Vec3;

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub(crate) struct Body {
    pub id: u32,
    pub position: Vec3,
    pub velocity: Vec3,
    pub gravity: f32,
    pub bounce: f32,
    pub radius: f32,
}

fn collide(a: &mut Body, b: &mut Body) -> (u32, bool) {
    let delta = b.position - a.position;
    let radii = a.radius + b.radius;
    let squared = delta.length_squared();
    if squared >= radii * radii {
        return (0, false);
    }
    let distance = squared.sqrt();
    let normal = if distance > 1e-6 {
        delta / distance
    } else {
        Vec3::X
    };
    let correction = normal * ((radii - distance) * 0.5 + 1e-5);
    a.position -= correction;
    b.position += correction;
    let closing_speed = (b.velocity - a.velocity).dot(normal);
    if closing_speed >= 0.0 {
        return (0, true);
    }
    let impulse = normal * (-(1.0 + 0.75) * closing_speed * 0.5);
    a.velocity -= impulse;
    b.velocity += impulse;
    (1, true)
}

pub(crate) fn simulate(
    bodies: &mut [Body],
    order: &mut Vec<usize>,
    pairs: &mut Vec<(usize, usize)>,
    dt: f32,
) -> u32 {
    if bodies.is_empty() {
        return 0;
    }
    let duration = dt.min(0.25);
    let steps = (duration / (1.0 / 240.0)).ceil().max(1.0) as u32;
    let step = duration / steps as f32;
    let friction = (-0.9 * step).exp();
    let max_radius = bodies.iter().map(|b| b.radius).fold(0.0, f32::max);
    let mut contacts: u32 = 0;
    order.clear();
    order.extend(0..bodies.len());
    for _ in 0..steps {
        for body in bodies.iter_mut() {
            body.velocity.y -= body.gravity * step;
            body.position += body.velocity * step;
            if body.position.y < body.radius {
                body.position.y = body.radius;
                if body.velocity.y < 0.0 {
                    body.velocity.y = -body.velocity.y * body.bounce;
                }
                body.velocity.x *= friction;
                body.velocity.z *= friction;
            }
        }
        // Corrections can create secondary contacts; rebuild the broad phase.
        for _ in 0..4 {
            let mut corrected = false;
            if bodies.len() <= 16 {
                // Tiny groups are cheaper without sorting or a spatial index.
                for i in 0..bodies.len() {
                    let (before, after) = bodies.split_at_mut(i + 1);
                    for b in after {
                        let (hits, moved) = collide(&mut before[i], b);
                        contacts = contacts.saturating_add(hits);
                        corrected |= moved;
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // Sweep sorted X bounds; build candidates before any contact correction.
                order.sort_unstable_by(|&a, &b| {
                    bodies[a].position.x.total_cmp(&bodies[b].position.x)
                });
                pairs.clear();
                for (offset, &a) in order.iter().enumerate() {
                    for &b in &order[offset + 1..] {
                        let delta = bodies[b].position - bodies[a].position;
                        if delta.x > bodies[a].radius + max_radius {
                            break;
                        }
                        let reach = bodies[a].radius + bodies[b].radius;
                        if delta.x > reach || delta.y.abs() > reach || delta.z.abs() > reach {
                            continue;
                        }
                        pairs.push((a.min(b), a.max(b)));
                    }
                }
                // Stable ID order keeps collision results independent of sorting ties.
                pairs.sort_unstable();
                for &(a, b) in pairs.iter() {
                    let (before, after) = bodies.split_at_mut(b);
                    let (hits, moved) = collide(&mut before[a], &mut after[0]);
                    contacts = contacts.saturating_add(hits);
                    corrected |= moved;
                }
            }
            if !corrected {
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    contacts
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn sweep_rebuilds_after_correction_creates_secondary_contact() {
        let mut bodies: Vec<_> = (0..17)
            .map(|id| Body {
                id,
                position: Vec3::new(
                    match id {
                        0 => 0.0,
                        1 => 1.0,
                        2 => 3.1,
                        _ => id as f32 * 10.0,
                    },
                    4.0,
                    0.0,
                ),
                velocity: Vec3::ZERO,
                gravity: 0.0,
                bounce: 0.0,
                radius: 1.0,
            })
            .collect();
        simulate(&mut bodies, &mut Vec::new(), &mut Vec::new(), 1.0 / 240.0);
        assert!((bodies[2].position - bodies[1].position).length() >= 2.0 - 1e-4);
    }
    #[test]
    fn sweep_keeps_contacts_across_mixed_radii_and_equal_x() {
        let mut bodies: Vec<_> = (0..40)
            .map(|i| Body {
                id: i,
                position: Vec3::new((i / 2) as f32 * 4.0, 4.0 + (i % 2) as f32 * 0.4, 0.0),
                velocity: if i % 2 == 0 { Vec3::Y } else { -Vec3::Y },
                radius: if i % 2 == 0 { 0.2 } else { 0.5 },
                gravity: 0.0,
                bounce: 0.0,
            })
            .collect();
        let contacts = simulate(&mut bodies, &mut Vec::new(), &mut Vec::new(), 1.0 / 240.0);
        assert_eq!(contacts, 20);
        for pair in bodies.as_chunks::<2>().0 {
            assert!((pair[1].position - pair[0].position).length() >= 0.7 - 1e-5);
        }
    }
}


# File: src/render/cutting_compute.rs
//! WebGPU geometry preparation only; topology remains owned by `cutting`.
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

use js_sys::Promise;
use wasm_bindgen::JsValue;
use wasm_bindgen_futures::JsFuture;
use wgpu::util::DeviceExt;

use crate::cutting::CutGeometryInput;

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
struct Parameters {
    plane: [f32; 4],
    counts: [u32; 4],
}

pub struct ComputeCutter {
    pipeline: wgpu::ComputePipeline,
    positions: HashMap<u32, wgpu::Buffer>,
}

/// Owns request-local readback resources; no mapped buffer enters the cache.
pub struct PendingCutGeometry {
    staging: Option<wgpu::Buffer>,
    rows: Vec<usize>,
    error: Option<String>,
}

// Also unmaps if a caller cancels `read` while its Promise is pending.
struct Mapping(wgpu::Buffer);

impl Drop for Mapping {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.0.unmap();
    }
}

impl ComputeCutter {
    pub fn retain(&mut self, alive: impl Fn(u32) -> bool) {
        self.positions.retain(|&id, _| alive(id));
    }
    pub fn new(device: &wgpu::Device) -> Self {
        let shader = device.create_shader_module(wgpu::ShaderModuleDescriptor {
            label: Some("cut geometry preparation"),
            source: wgpu::ShaderSource::Wgsl(include_str!("cutting_compute.wgsl").into()),
        });
        let pipeline = device.create_compute_pipeline(&wgpu::ComputePipelineDescriptor {
            label: Some("cut geometry preparation"),
            layout: None,
            module: &shader,
            entry_point: Some("prepare"),
            compilation_options: Default::default(),
            cache: None,
        });
        Self {
            pipeline,
            positions: HashMap::new(),
        }
    }

    /// Mesh IDs identify immutable position arrays. Edges and planes are per request.
    /// Only caller-supplied BVH candidates are evaluated, in their original order.
    pub fn dispatch(
        &mut self,
        device: &wgpu::Device,
        queue: &wgpu::Queue,
        inputs: &[CutGeometryInput],
    ) -> PendingCutGeometry {
        let ids: HashSet<_> = inputs.iter().map(|input| input.id).collect();
        self.positions.retain(|id, _| ids.contains(id));
        let mut pending = PendingCutGeometry {
            staging: None,
            rows: Vec::with_capacity(inputs.len()),
            error: None,
        };
        let limits = device.limits();
        let storage_limit = limits
            .max_storage_buffer_binding_size
            .min(limits.max_buffer_size);
        let mut total_bytes = 0u64;
        // Validate the entire batch before allocating or submitting any work.
        for input in inputs {
            let vertices = input.positions.len() as u64;
            let edges = input.edges.len() as u64;
            let rows = vertices + edges;
            let output_bytes = rows * 16;
            if rows == 0 {
                pending.rows.push(0);
                continue;
            }
            if vertices > u64::from(u32::MAX)
                || edges > u64::from(u32::MAX)
                || rows > u64::from(u32::MAX)
                || output_bytes > storage_limit
                || (vertices * 16).max(16) > storage_limit
                || (edges * 8).max(8) > storage_limit
                || limits.max_compute_workgroups_per_dimension == 0
                || limits.max_compute_invocations_per_workgroup < 64
                || limits.max_compute_workgroup_size_x < 64
                || limits.max_storage_buffers_per_shader_stage < 3
            {
                pending.error = Some(format!(
                    "Cut geometry input {} exceeds device limits",
                    input.id
                ));
                return pending;
            }
            let groups = (rows as u32).div_ceil(64);
            let x = groups.min(limits.max_compute_workgroups_per_dimension);
            if groups.div_ceil(x) > limits.max_compute_workgroups_per_dimension {
                pending.error = Some("Cut geometry dispatch exceeds workgroup limits".into());
                return pending;
            }
            total_bytes += output_bytes;
            if total_bytes > limits.max_buffer_size {
                pending.error = Some("Cut geometry readback exceeds device buffer limit".into());
                return pending;
            }
            pending.rows.push(rows as usize);
        }
        if total_bytes == 0 {
            return pending;
        }

        let staging = device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
            label: Some("cut geometry batched readback"),
            size: total_bytes,
            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST | wgpu::BufferUsages::MAP_READ,
            mapped_at_creation: false,
        });
        let mut encoder = device.create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor {
            label: Some("cut geometry batch"),
        });
        let layout = self.pipeline.get_bind_group_layout(0);
        let mut offset = 0u64;
        for (input, &rows) in inputs.iter().zip(&pending.rows) {
            if rows == 0 {
                continue;
            }
            let positions = self.positions.entry(input.id).or_insert_with(|| {
                let fallback = [[0.0f32; 4]];
                let values = if input.positions.is_empty() {
                    &fallback[..]
                } else {
                    &input.positions[..]
                };
                device.create_buffer_init(&wgpu::util::BufferInitDescriptor {
                    label: Some("cut immutable mesh positions"),
                    contents: bytemuck::cast_slice(values),
                    usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE,
                })
            });
            let canonical_edges: Vec<[u32; 2]> = input
                .edges
                .iter()
                .map(|&[i, j]| [i.min(j), i.max(j)])
                .collect();
            let edge_values = if canonical_edges.is_empty() {
                &[[0u32; 2]][..]
            } else {
                &canonical_edges[..]
            };
            let edges = device.create_buffer_init(&wgpu::util::BufferInitDescriptor {
                label: Some("cut candidate edges"),
                contents: bytemuck::cast_slice(edge_values),
                usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE,
            });
            let parameters = device.create_buffer_init(&wgpu::util::BufferInitDescriptor {
                label: Some("cut plane and counts"),
                contents: bytemuck::bytes_of(&Parameters {
                    plane: input.plane,
                    counts: [input.positions.len() as u32, input.edges.len() as u32, 0, 0],
                }),
                usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::UNIFORM,
            });
            let bytes = rows as u64 * 16;
            let output = device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
                label: Some("cut geometry output"),
                size: bytes,
                usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_SRC,
                mapped_at_creation: false,
            });
            let bind_group = device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor {
                label: Some("cut geometry bindings"),
                layout: &layout,
                entries: &[
                    wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                        binding: 0,
                        resource: positions.as_entire_binding(),
                    },
                    wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                        binding: 1,
                        resource: edges.as_entire_binding(),
                    },
                    wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                        binding: 2,
                        resource: output.as_entire_binding(),
                    },
                    wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                        binding: 3,
                        resource: parameters.as_entire_binding(),
                    },
                ],
            });
            let groups = (rows as u32).div_ceil(64);
            let x = groups.min(limits.max_compute_workgroups_per_dimension);
            let y = groups.div_ceil(x);
            {
                let mut pass = encoder.begin_compute_pass(&wgpu::ComputePassDescriptor {
                    label: Some("cut geometry preparation"),
                    timestamp_writes: None,
                });
                pass.set_pipeline(&self.pipeline);
                pass.set_bind_group(0, &bind_group, &[]);
                pass.dispatch_workgroups(x, y, 1);
            }
            encoder.copy_buffer_to_buffer(&output, 0, &staging, offset, bytes);
            offset += bytes;
        }
        queue.submit([encoder.finish()]);
        pending.staging = Some(staging);
        pending
    }
}

impl PendingCutGeometry {
    pub async fn read(self) -> Result<Vec<Vec<[f32; 4]>>, String> {
        if let Some(error) = self.error {
            return Err(error);
        }
        let Some(staging) = self.staging else {
            return Ok(self.rows.iter().map(|_| Vec::new()).collect());
        };
        let mapping = Mapping(staging);
        let promise = Promise::new(&mut |resolve, reject| {
            mapping
                .0
                .slice(..)
                .map_async(wgpu::MapMode::Read, move |result| match result {
                    Ok(()) => {
                        let _ = resolve.call0(&JsValue::UNDEFINED);
                    }
                    Err(error) => {
                        let _ = reject
                            .call1(&JsValue::UNDEFINED, &JsValue::from_str(&error.to_string()));
                    }
                });
        });
        JsFuture::from(promise).await.map_err(|error| {
            error
                .as_string()
                .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("Cut geometry mapping failed: {error:?}"))
        })?;
        let view = mapping
            .0
            .slice(..)
            .get_mapped_range()
            .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
        let values: &[[f32; 4]] =
            bytemuck::try_cast_slice(&view).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
        let mut offset = 0;
        let mut results = Vec::with_capacity(self.rows.len());
        for rows in self.rows {
            results.push(values[offset..offset + rows].to_vec());
            offset += rows;
        }
        drop(view);
        drop(mapping);
        Ok(results)
    }
}


# File: src/render/cutting_compute.wgsl
struct Parameters {
    plane: vec4<f32>,
    counts: vec4<u32>, // vertices, candidate edges, unused, unused
}

@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> positions: array<vec4<f32>>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> edges: array<vec2<u32>>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> results: array<vec4<f32>>;
@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> parameters: Parameters;

fn signed_distance(position: vec3<f32>) -> f32 {
    let distance = dot(parameters.plane.xyz, position) + parameters.plane.w;
    return select(distance, 0.0, abs(distance) < 1e-5);
}

@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn prepare(
    @builtin(workgroup_id) group: vec3<u32>,
    @builtin(num_workgroups) groups: vec3<u32>,
    @builtin(local_invocation_index) lane: u32,
) {
    let row = (group.y * groups.x + group.x) * 64u + lane;
    let vertex_count = parameters.counts.x;
    let edge_count = parameters.counts.y;
    if row >= vertex_count + edge_count || row >= arrayLength(&results) {
        return;
    }
    if row < vertex_count {
        if row < arrayLength(&positions) {
            results[row] = vec4<f32>(signed_distance(positions[row].xyz), 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
        }
        return;
    }

    results[row] = vec4<f32>(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, -1.0);
    let edge_index = row - vertex_count;
    if edge_index >= edge_count || edge_index >= arrayLength(&edges) {
        return;
    }
    let edge = edges[edge_index];
    let i = min(edge.x, edge.y);
    let j = max(edge.x, edge.y);
    if i >= vertex_count || j >= vertex_count || j >= arrayLength(&positions) {
        return;
    }
    let pi = positions[i].xyz;
    let pj = positions[j].xyz;
    // Recompute here: another invocation's distance row is not synchronized.
    let di = signed_distance(pi);
    let dj = signed_distance(pj);
    if (di < 0.0 && dj > 0.0) || (di > 0.0 && dj < 0.0) {
        let t = di / (di - dj);
        results[row] = vec4<f32>(pi + (pj - pi) * t, t);
    }
}


# File: src/render/cutting_resident.rs
//! Request-local GPU cutting. Successful reads transfer metadata only (640 bytes).
//! The caller owns eligibility, immutable source uploads, and transactional scene changes.
use glam::Vec3;
use js_sys::Promise;
use std::{future::Future, pin::Pin};
use wasm_bindgen::JsValue;
use wasm_bindgen_futures::JsFuture;
use wgpu::util::DeviceExt;

const MAX_TRIANGLES: u32 = 20_000;
pub const RESIDENT_METADATA_BYTES: u64 = 640;
type ErrorFutures = Vec<Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Option<wgpu::Error>>>>>;

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
pub struct ResidentVertex {
    pub position: [f32; 4],
    pub normal: [f32; 4],
    pub color: [f32; 4],
}

pub struct ResidentSource {
    /// Immutable triangle soup; requires STORAGE and COPY_SRC usages.
    pub vertices: wgpu::Buffer,
    pub vertex_count: u32,
}

pub struct ResidentCutParams {
    pub a: Vec3,
    pub b: Vec3,
    pub motion: Vec3,
    pub cap_color: Vec3,
    pub source_volume: f32,
    /// First 64 directions address the positive half, next 64 the negative half.
    /// Supports use the same local coordinates as `source`, without recentering.
    pub support_dirs: [[f32; 4]; 128],
}

pub struct ResidentPiece {
    pub source: ResidentSource,
    pub render_vertices: wgpu::Buffer,
    pub indices: wgpu::Buffer,
    pub vertex_count: u32,
    pub bounds_min: Vec3,
    pub bounds_max: Vec3,
    pub volume: f32,
    pub support: [f32; 64],
}

pub enum ResidentOutcome {
    Miss,
    Unsupported(String),
    Pieces(Box<[ResidentPiece; 2]>),
}

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
struct Parameters {
    plane: [f32; 4],
    a: [f32; 4],
    blade: [f32; 4],
    motion: [f32; 4],
    color: [f32; 4],
    // input triangles, per-half vertex capacity, segment capacity, hash slots
    counts: [u32; 4],
    directions: [[f32; 4]; 128],
}

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
struct Metadata {
    count: u32,
    segments: u32,
    status: u32,
    sides: u32,
    lo: [f32; 4],
    hi: [f32; 4],
    volume: [f32; 4],
    support: [f32; 64],
}

const _: () = {
    assert!(std::mem::size_of::<ResidentVertex>() == 48);
    assert!(std::mem::size_of::<Metadata>() * 2 == RESIDENT_METADATA_BYTES as usize);
    assert!(std::mem::size_of::<Parameters>() == 2144);
};

pub struct ResidentCutter {
    layout: wgpu::BindGroupLayout,
    pipelines: [wgpu::ComputePipeline; 5],
    initialization_errors: std::cell::RefCell<ErrorFutures>,
}

pub struct PendingResidentCut {
    staging: wgpu::Buffer,
    output: wgpu::Buffer,
    packed: wgpu::Buffer,
    // Explicit ownership until read/cancellation; command buffers also retain these.
    _retained: [wgpu::Buffer; 4],
    capacity: u32,
    source_volume: f32,
    errors: Vec<Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Option<wgpu::Error>>>>>,
}

// Pop immediately before returning control to the renderer: an outstanding
// request must never leave scopes installed on the shared device.
fn error_scopes(device: &wgpu::Device) -> [wgpu::ErrorScopeGuard; 3] {
    [
        device.push_error_scope(wgpu::ErrorFilter::OutOfMemory),
        device.push_error_scope(wgpu::ErrorFilter::Internal),
        device.push_error_scope(wgpu::ErrorFilter::Validation),
    ]
}

fn pop_scopes(
    scopes: [wgpu::ErrorScopeGuard; 3],
) -> Vec<Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Option<wgpu::Error>>>>> {
    scopes
        .into_iter()
        .rev()
        .map(|scope| Box::pin(scope.pop()) as Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Option<wgpu::Error>>>>)
        .collect()
}

async fn check_errors(
    errors: Vec<Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Option<wgpu::Error>>>>>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    for error in errors {
        if let Some(error) = error.await {
            return Err(format!("Resident cutting GPU error: {error}"));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

struct Mapping(wgpu::Buffer);
impl Drop for Mapping {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.0.unmap();
    }
}

fn buffer(
    device: &wgpu::Device,
    label: &str,
    size: u64,
    usage: wgpu::BufferUsages,
) -> wgpu::Buffer {
    device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
        label: Some(label),
        size,
        usage,
        mapped_at_creation: false,
    })
}

async fn mapped_bytes(staging: wgpu::Buffer) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    // RAII unmaps even if this future is dropped while map_async is pending.
    let mapping = Mapping(staging);
    let promise = Promise::new(&mut |resolve, reject| {
        mapping
            .0
            .slice(..)
            .map_async(wgpu::MapMode::Read, move |result| match result {
                Ok(()) => {
                    let _ = resolve.call0(&JsValue::UNDEFINED);
                }
                Err(error) => {
                    let _ =
                        reject.call1(&JsValue::UNDEFINED, &JsValue::from_str(&error.to_string()));
                }
            });
    });
    JsFuture::from(promise).await.map_err(|error| {
        error
            .as_string()
            .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("Resident cut map failed: {error:?}"))
    })?;
    let view = mapping
        .0
        .slice(..)
        .get_mapped_range()
        .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    Ok(view.to_vec())
}

impl ResidentCutter {
    pub fn new(device: &wgpu::Device) -> Self {
        let scopes = error_scopes(device);
        let shader = device.create_shader_module(wgpu::ShaderModuleDescriptor {
            label: Some("resident cutting"),
            source: wgpu::ShaderSource::Wgsl(include_str!("cutting_resident.wgsl").into()),
        });
        let entries: Vec<_> = (0..6)
            .map(|binding| wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                binding,
                visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::COMPUTE,
                ty: wgpu::BindingType::Buffer {
                    ty: if binding == 5 {
                        wgpu::BufferBindingType::Uniform
                    } else {
                        wgpu::BufferBindingType::Storage {
                            read_only: binding == 0,
                        }
                    },
                    has_dynamic_offset: false,
                    min_binding_size: None,
                },
                count: None,
            })
            .collect();
        let layout = device.create_bind_group_layout(&wgpu::BindGroupLayoutDescriptor {
            label: Some("resident cutting"),
            entries: &entries,
        });
        let pipeline_layout = device.create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor {
            label: Some("resident cutting"),
            bind_group_layouts: &[Some(&layout)],
            immediate_size: 0,
        });
        let pipelines = ["clip", "close_boundary", "measure", "support", "pack"].map(|entry| {
            device.create_compute_pipeline(&wgpu::ComputePipelineDescriptor {
                label: Some(entry),
                layout: Some(&pipeline_layout),
                module: &shader,
                entry_point: Some(entry),
                compilation_options: Default::default(),
                cache: None,
            })
        });
        Self {
            layout,
            pipelines,
            initialization_errors: std::cell::RefCell::new(pop_scopes(scopes)),
        }
    }

    pub fn dispatch(
        &self,
        device: &wgpu::Device,
        queue: &wgpu::Queue,
        source: &ResidentSource,
        params: &ResidentCutParams,
    ) -> Result<PendingResidentCut, String> {
        let count = source.vertex_count;
        if count == 0 || !count.is_multiple_of(3) || count / 3 > MAX_TRIANGLES {
            return Err("Resident source must contain 1..20000 complete triangles".into());
        }
        if !source
            .vertices
            .usage()
            .contains(wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_SRC)
            || source.vertices.size() < u64::from(count) * 48
        {
            return Err(
                "Resident source requires a sufficiently large STORAGE | COPY_SRC buffer".into(),
            );
        }
        let blade = params.b - params.a;
        let cross = blade.cross(params.motion);
        if !params.a.is_finite()
            || !params.b.is_finite()
            || !params.motion.is_finite()
            || !params.cap_color.is_finite()
            || !cross.is_finite()
            || cross.length() < 1e-5
            || !params.source_volume.is_finite()
            || params.source_volume <= 0.0
            || params.support_dirs.iter().flatten().any(|v| !v.is_finite())
        {
            return Err("Resident cut parameters must be finite, with a nondegenerate sweep and positive volume".into());
        }
        let normal = cross.normalize();
        let plane_d = normal.dot(params.a);
        let uu = blade.length_squared();
        let vv = params.motion.length_squared();
        let uv = blade.dot(params.motion);
        let det = uu * vv - uv * uv;
        if !plane_d.is_finite() || !det.is_finite() || det <= (uu * vv) * 1e-6 {
            return Err("Resident sweep is numerically ill-conditioned".into());
        }
        let triangles = count / 3;
        // Quest-oriented bounded boundary scratch: excessive contour complexity
        // falls back atomically instead of allocating tens of MB per apple.
        let segments = (triangles * 3).min(4096);
        let capacity = (triangles * 9).min(triangles * 3 + segments * 6);
        let hash = (segments * 2).next_power_of_two();
        let output_bytes = u64::from(capacity) * 2 * 48;
        let scratch_bytes = u64::from(segments + 2 * hash) * 2 * 48;
        // Each half is [render: capacity * 36][indices: capacity * 4].
        let packed_bytes = u64::from(capacity) * 2 * 40;
        let limits = device.limits();
        let max_binding = limits
            .max_storage_buffer_binding_size
            .min(limits.max_buffer_size);
        if [
            output_bytes,
            scratch_bytes,
            packed_bytes,
            source.vertices.size(),
        ]
        .iter()
        .any(|&bytes| bytes > max_binding)
            || limits.max_storage_buffers_per_shader_stage < 5
            || limits.max_compute_workgroup_size_x < 64
            || limits.max_compute_invocations_per_workgroup < 64
            || limits.max_compute_workgroups_per_dimension < capacity.div_ceil(64).max(128)
            || limits.max_uniform_buffer_binding_size < std::mem::size_of::<Parameters>() as u64
        {
            return Err("Resident cutting exceeds device compute/buffer limits".into());
        }
        let scopes = error_scopes(device);
        let storage = wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_SRC;
        let output = buffer(device, "resident cut soup scratch", output_bytes, storage);
        let scratch = buffer(
            device,
            "resident cut boundary scratch",
            scratch_bytes,
            wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE,
        );
        let packed = buffer(device, "resident cut packed scratch", packed_bytes, storage);
        let metadata = buffer(
            device,
            "resident cut metadata",
            RESIDENT_METADATA_BYTES,
            storage,
        );
        let staging = buffer(
            device,
            "resident cut metadata readback",
            RESIDENT_METADATA_BYTES,
            wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST | wgpu::BufferUsages::MAP_READ,
        );
        let uniform = device.create_buffer_init(&wgpu::util::BufferInitDescriptor {
            label: Some("resident cut parameters"),
            contents: bytemuck::bytes_of(&Parameters {
                plane: [normal.x, normal.y, normal.z, plane_d],
                a: params
                    .a
                    .extend(
                        if params.support_dirs[..64]
                            .iter()
                            .all(|d| *d == params.support_dirs[0])
                            && params.support_dirs[64..]
                                .iter()
                                .all(|d| *d == params.support_dirs[64])
                        {
                            1.
                        } else {
                            64.
                        },
                    )
                    .to_array(),
                blade: blade.extend(0.0).to_array(),
                motion: params.motion.extend(0.0).to_array(),
                color: params.cap_color.extend(1.0).to_array(),
                counts: [triangles, capacity, segments, hash],
                directions: params.support_dirs,
            }),
            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::UNIFORM,
        });
        let buffers = [
            &source.vertices,
            &output,
            &scratch,
            &metadata,
            &packed,
            &uniform,
        ];
        let entries: Vec<_> = buffers
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(i, b)| wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                binding: i as u32,
                resource: b.as_entire_binding(),
            })
            .collect();
        let group = device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor {
            label: Some("resident cut request"),
            layout: &self.layout,
            entries: &entries,
        });
        let mut encoder = device.create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor {
            label: Some("resident cut phases"),
        });
        // Distinct passes provide storage visibility between globally dependent phases.
        for (pipeline, groups) in self.pipelines.iter().zip([
            [triangles.div_ceil(64), 1],
            [2, 1],
            [2, 1],
            [128, 1],
            [capacity.div_ceil(64), 2],
        ]) {
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_compute_pass(&wgpu::ComputePassDescriptor {
                label: Some("resident cut phase"),
                timestamp_writes: None,
            });
            pass.set_pipeline(pipeline);
            pass.set_bind_group(0, &group, &[]);
            pass.dispatch_workgroups(groups[0], groups[1], 1);
        }
        encoder.copy_buffer_to_buffer(&metadata, 0, &staging, 0, RESIDENT_METADATA_BYTES);
        queue.submit([encoder.finish()]);
        let mut errors = std::mem::take(&mut *self.initialization_errors.borrow_mut());
        errors.extend(pop_scopes(scopes));
        Ok(PendingResidentCut {
            staging,
            output,
            packed,
            _retained: [source.vertices.clone(), scratch, metadata, uniform],
            capacity,
            source_volume: params.source_volume,
            errors,
        })
    }
}

impl PendingResidentCut {
    pub async fn read(
        self,
        device: &wgpu::Device,
        queue: &wgpu::Queue,
    ) -> Result<ResidentOutcome, String> {
        check_errors(self.errors).await?;
        let bytes = mapped_bytes(self.staging).await?;
        let metadata: [Metadata; 2] = bytemuck::pod_read_unaligned(&bytes);
        // Unsupported takes precedence over miss: a damaged source must not be accepted.
        if let Some(m) = metadata.iter().find(|m| m.status >= 2) {
            let reason = match m.status {
                2 => "ambiguous, nonmanifold or multiple cap contours",
                3 => "triangle, segment or hash budget exceeded",
                4 => "nonfinite or near-EPS numerically ambiguous geometry",
                5 => "cap contour has no safe average-center fan",
                _ => "unknown GPU status",
            };
            return Ok(ResidentOutcome::Unsupported(reason.into()));
        }
        if metadata
            .iter()
            .any(|m| m.status == 1 || m.sides != 3 || m.count == 0)
        {
            return Ok(ResidentOutcome::Miss);
        }
        if metadata.iter().any(|m| m.count / 3 > MAX_TRIANGLES) {
            return Ok(ResidentOutcome::Unsupported(
                "Resident piece exceeds the CPU-fallback triangle budget".into(),
            ));
        }
        for m in &metadata {
            if m.count > self.capacity
                || !m.count.is_multiple_of(3)
                || m.lo
                    .iter()
                    .chain(&m.hi)
                    .chain(&m.volume)
                    .chain(&m.support)
                    .any(|v| !v.is_finite())
                || (0..3).any(|i| m.lo[i] > m.hi[i])
                || m.volume[0] <= 0.0
            {
                return Ok(ResidentOutcome::Unsupported(
                    "Invalid resident metadata".into(),
                ));
            }
        }
        let total = metadata[0].volume[0] + metadata[1].volume[0];
        if (total - self.source_volume).abs() > self.source_volume * 0.001 {
            return Ok(ResidentOutcome::Unsupported(
                "Resident volume conservation exceeded 0.001 relative tolerance".into(),
            ));
        }
        let scopes = error_scopes(device);
        let mut encoder = device.create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor {
            label: Some("resident cut exact GPU copies"),
        });
        let pieces = std::array::from_fn(|half| {
            let m = metadata[half];
            let count = u64::from(m.count);
            let soup = buffer(
                device,
                "resident immutable piece soup",
                count * 48,
                wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE
                    | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST
                    | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_SRC,
            );
            let render = buffer(
                device,
                "resident piece vertices",
                count * 36,
                wgpu::BufferUsages::VERTEX | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST,
            );
            let indices = buffer(
                device,
                "resident piece indices",
                count * 4,
                wgpu::BufferUsages::INDEX | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST,
            );
            let base = half as u64 * u64::from(self.capacity);
            encoder.copy_buffer_to_buffer(&self.output, base * 48, &soup, 0, count * 48);
            encoder.copy_buffer_to_buffer(&self.packed, base * 40, &render, 0, count * 36);
            encoder.copy_buffer_to_buffer(
                &self.packed,
                base * 40 + u64::from(self.capacity) * 36,
                &indices,
                0,
                count * 4,
            );
            ResidentPiece {
                source: ResidentSource {
                    vertices: soup,
                    vertex_count: m.count,
                },
                render_vertices: render,
                indices,
                vertex_count: m.count,
                bounds_min: Vec3::from_slice(&m.lo[..3]),
                bounds_max: Vec3::from_slice(&m.hi[..3]),
                volume: m.volume[0],
                support: m.support,
            }
        });
        queue.submit([encoder.finish()]);
        check_errors(pop_scopes(scopes)).await?;
        Ok(ResidentOutcome::Pieces(Box::new(pieces)))
    }
}

impl ResidentSource {
    /// Explicit diagnostic geometry readback; never used by the successful cut path.
    pub async fn read_vertices(
        &self,
        device: &wgpu::Device,
        queue: &wgpu::Queue,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ResidentVertex>, String> {
        if self.vertex_count == 0 {
            return Ok(Vec::new());
        }
        let size = u64::from(self.vertex_count) * 48;
        if size > self.vertices.size()
            || !self.vertices.usage().contains(wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_SRC)
        {
            return Err("Resident diagnostic source is not copyable or is truncated".into());
        }
        let scopes = error_scopes(device);
        let staging = buffer(
            device,
            "resident diagnostic geometry readback",
            size,
            wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST | wgpu::BufferUsages::MAP_READ,
        );
        let mut encoder = device.create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor {
            label: Some("resident diagnostics"),
        });
        encoder.copy_buffer_to_buffer(&self.vertices, 0, &staging, 0, size);
        queue.submit([encoder.finish()]);
        check_errors(pop_scopes(scopes)).await?;
        let bytes = mapped_bytes(staging).await?;
        Ok(bytes
            .as_chunks::<48>()
            .0
            .iter()
            .map(|bytes| bytemuck::pod_read_unaligned(bytes))
            .collect())
    }
}


# File: src/render/cutting_resident.wgsl
// Five storage bindings, fixed request budgets, no CPU geometry round trip.
struct Vertex { position: vec4<f32>, normal: vec4<f32>, color: vec4<f32> }
struct Parameters {
    plane: vec4<f32>, a: vec4<f32>, blade: vec4<f32>, motion: vec4<f32>, color: vec4<f32>,
    counts: vec4<u32>, directions: array<vec4<f32>, 128>,
}
struct Metadata {
    count: atomic<u32>, segments: atomic<u32>, status: atomic<u32>, sides: atomic<u32>,
    lo: vec4<f32>, hi: vec4<f32>, volume: vec4<f32>, samples: array<f32, 64>,
}
// Segment region, undirected edge hash, directed point hash (per half).
// Segment region is reused for ordered loop indices after constructing the hashes.
struct Edge { a: vec4<f32>, b: vec4<f32>, count: u32, next: u32, other: u32, pad: u32 }
@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage, read> source: array<Vertex>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read_write> output: array<Vertex>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read_write> scratch: array<Edge>;
@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read_write> metadata: array<Metadata>;
@group(0) @binding(4) var<storage, read_write> packed: array<u32>;
@group(0) @binding(5) var<uniform> p: Parameters;
const EPS: f32 = 0.00001;
const BIG: f32 = 3.402823e38;

fn fail(h: u32, code: u32) { atomicMax(&metadata[h].status, code); }
fn finite(v: vec3<f32>) -> bool { return all(abs(v) <= vec3<f32>(BIG)); }
fn equal(a: vec3<f32>, b: vec3<f32>) -> bool { return all(a == b); }
fn less(a: vec3<f32>, b: vec3<f32>) -> bool {
    if a.x != b.x { return a.x < b.x; }
    if a.y != b.y { return a.y < b.y; }
    return a.z < b.z;
}
fn distance(v: vec3<f32>) -> f32 { return dot(p.plane.xyz, v) - p.plane.w; }
fn classified(v: vec3<f32>) -> f32 {
    let d = distance(v);
    return select(d, 0.0, abs(d) < EPS);
}
fn uncertain(v: vec3<f32>) -> bool {
    let d = distance(v);
    // Conservative dot/subtraction rounding bound; do not invent near-plane topology.
    let error = (dot(abs(p.plane.xyz), abs(v)) + abs(p.plane.w)) * 0.0000005;
    return !finite(v) || !(abs(d) <= BIG) ||
        (abs(d) < EPS && (d != 0.0 || error > EPS * 0.25)) || abs(abs(d) - EPS) <= error;
}
fn interpolate(v0: Vertex, v1: Vertex) -> Vertex {
    var a = v0; var b = v1;
    if less(b.position.xyz, a.position.xyz) { a = v1; b = v0; }
    let da = classified(a.position.xyz); let db = classified(b.position.xyz);
    let t = da / (da - db);
    return Vertex(vec4<f32>(a.position.xyz + (b.position.xyz - a.position.xyz) * t, 1.0),
        a.normal + (b.normal - a.normal) * t, a.color + (b.color - a.color) * t);
}
fn scratch_base(h: u32) -> u32 { return h * (p.counts.z + 2u * p.counts.w); }
fn triangle(h: u32, a: Vertex, b: Vertex, c: Vertex) {
    if !finite(a.position.xyz) || !finite(b.position.xyz) || !finite(c.position.xyz) ||
        !finite(a.normal.xyz) || !finite(b.normal.xyz) || !finite(c.normal.xyz) ||
        !finite(a.color.xyz) || !finite(b.color.xyz) || !finite(c.color.xyz) {
        fail(h, 4u); return;
    }
    let i = atomicAdd(&metadata[h].count, 3u);
    if i + 3u > p.counts.y { fail(h, 3u); return; }
    let base = h * p.counts.y + i;
    output[base] = a; output[base + 1u] = b; output[base + 2u] = c;
}
fn segment(h: u32, a: vec3<f32>, b: vec3<f32>) {
    if equal(a, b) { fail(h, 2u); return; }
    let i = atomicAdd(&metadata[h].segments, 1u);
    if i >= p.counts.z { fail(h, 3u); return; }
    scratch[scratch_base(h) + i] = Edge(vec4<f32>(a, 0.0), vec4<f32>(b, 0.0), 0u, 0u, 0u, 0u);
}

@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn clip(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {
    if gid.x >= p.counts.x { return; }
    let start = gid.x * 3u;
    var v: array<Vertex, 3>;
    var d: array<f32, 3>;
    var sides = 0u;
    for (var k = 0u; k < 3u; k++) {
        v[k] = source[start + k];
        if uncertain(v[k].position.xyz) || !finite(v[k].normal.xyz) || !finite(v[k].color.xyz) {
            fail(0u, 4u); fail(1u, 4u); return;
        }
        d[k] = classified(v[k].position.xyz);
        if d[k] > EPS { sides |= 1u; }
        if d[k] < -EPS { sides |= 2u; }
    }
    atomicOr(&metadata[0].sides, sides); atomicOr(&metadata[1].sides, sides);
    let coplanar = d[0] == 0.0 && d[1] == 0.0 && d[2] == 0.0;
    let owner = select(0u, 1u, dot(cross(v[1].position.xyz - v[0].position.xyz,
        v[2].position.xyz - v[0].position.xyz), p.plane.xyz) > 0.0);
    for (var h = 0u; h < 2u; h++) {
        if coplanar && owner != h { continue; }
        let sign = select(1.0, -1.0, h == 1u);
        var poly: array<Vertex, 4>;
        var on: array<bool, 4>;
        var n = 0u;
        for (var k = 0u; k < 3u; k++) {
            let j = (k + 1u) % 3u;
            if d[k] * sign >= 0.0 { poly[n] = v[k]; on[n] = d[k] == 0.0; n++; }
            if d[k] * d[j] < 0.0 {
                let vertex = interpolate(v[k], v[j]);
                let error = (dot(abs(p.plane.xyz), abs(vertex.position.xyz)) + abs(p.plane.w)) * 0.0000005;
                if error > EPS * 0.25 || abs(distance(vertex.position.xyz)) > EPS * 0.25 {
                    fail(0u, 4u); fail(1u, 4u); return;
                }
                poly[n] = vertex; on[n] = true; n++;
            }
        }
        if n < 3u { continue; }
        for (var k = 1u; k + 1u < n; k++) { triangle(h, poly[0], poly[k], poly[k + 1u]); }
        for (var k = 0u; k < n; k++) {
            let j = (k + 1u) % n;
            if on[k] && on[j] { segment(h, poly[k].position.xyz, poly[j].position.xyz); }
        }
    }
}

fn point_hash(a: vec3<f32>) -> u32 {
    // Floating equality equates -0/+0, so their hashes must agree as well.
    let bits = bitcast<vec3<u32>>(select(a, vec3<f32>(0.0), a == vec3<f32>(0.0)));
    var h = (bits.x * 0x9e3779b9u) ^ (bits.y * 0x85ebca6bu) ^ (bits.z * 0xc2b2ae35u);
    h ^= h >> 16u; h *= 0x7feb352du; h ^= h >> 15u;
    return h;
}
fn inside_sweep(a: vec3<f32>) -> bool {
    let q = a - p.a.xyz; let u = p.blade.xyz; let v = p.motion.xyz;
    let uu = dot(u, u); let vv = dot(v, v); let uv = dot(u, v);
    let det = uu * vv - uv * uv;
    let s = (dot(q, u) * vv - dot(q, v) * uv) / det;
    let t = (dot(q, v) * uu - dot(q, u) * uv) / det;
    return s >= -EPS && s <= 1.0 + EPS && t >= -EPS && t <= 1.0 + EPS;
}
fn point_lookup(base: u32, a: vec3<f32>) -> u32 {
    let mask = p.counts.w - 1u;
    var slot = point_hash(a) & mask;
    for (var probe = 0u; probe < p.counts.w; probe++) {
        let index = base + slot;
        if scratch[index].count == 0u { return 0xffffffffu; }
        if equal(scratch[index].a.xyz, a) { return index; }
        slot = (slot + 1u) & mask;
    }
    return 0xffffffffu;
}

@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn close_boundary(@builtin(workgroup_id) group: vec3<u32>, @builtin(local_invocation_index) lane: u32) {
    if lane != 0u { return; }
    let h = group.x;
    if atomicLoad(&metadata[h].status) != 0u { return; }
    if atomicLoad(&metadata[h].sides) != 3u { fail(h, 1u); return; }
    let base = scratch_base(h); let edges = base + p.counts.z; let points = edges + p.counts.w;
    let mask = p.counts.w - 1u;
    let count = atomicLoad(&metadata[h].segments);
    // Non-atomic hash: exactly one invocation owns this half in this phase.
    for (var i = 0u; i < count; i++) {
        let original = scratch[base + i];
        var a = original.a; var b = original.b;
        if less(b.xyz, a.xyz) { a = original.b; b = original.a; }
        var slot = (point_hash(a.xyz) ^ (point_hash(b.xyz) * 0x9e3779b9u)) & mask;
        var inserted = false;
        for (var probe = 0u; probe < p.counts.w; probe++) {
            let index = edges + slot;
            if scratch[index].count == 0u {
                // Preserve original orientation separately from the canonical key.
                scratch[index] = Edge(a, b, 1u, select(0u, 1u, equal(a.xyz, original.a.xyz)), 0u, 0u);
                inserted = true; break;
            }
            if equal(scratch[index].a.xyz, a.xyz) && equal(scratch[index].b.xyz, b.xyz) {
                if scratch[index].count != 1u || scratch[index].next == select(0u, 1u, equal(a.xyz, original.a.xyz)) {
                    fail(h, 2u); return;
                }
                scratch[index].count = 2u; inserted = true; break;
            }
            slot = (slot + 1u) & mask;
        }
        if !inserted { fail(h, 3u); return; }
    }
    var boundary_count = 0u; var first = 0xffffffffu;
    for (var i = 0u; i < p.counts.w; i++) {
        let e = scratch[edges + i];
        if e.count != 1u { continue; }
        // Reverse the exposed surface edge so cap winding closes the surface.
        let a = select(e.a, e.b, e.next == 1u); let b = select(e.b, e.a, e.next == 1u);
        if !inside_sweep(a.xyz) || !inside_sweep(b.xyz) { fail(h, 1u); return; }
        var slot = point_hash(a.xyz) & mask;
        var inserted = false;
        for (var probe = 0u; probe < p.counts.w; probe++) {
            let index = points + slot;
            if scratch[index].count == 0u {
                scratch[index] = Edge(a, b, 1u, 0u, 0u, 0u);
                if first == 0xffffffffu { first = index; }
                boundary_count++; inserted = true; break;
            }
            if equal(scratch[index].a.xyz, a.xyz) { fail(h, 2u); return; }
            slot = (slot + 1u) & mask;
        }
        if !inserted { fail(h, 3u); return; }
    }
    if boundary_count < 3u { fail(h, 1u); return; }
    // Traverse precisely one loop and mark each point. Additional contours fail.
    var current = first;
    let origin = scratch[first].a.xyz;
    var sum = vec3<f32>(0.0); var compensation = vec3<f32>(0.0);
    for (var i = 0u; i < boundary_count; i++) {
        if current == 0xffffffffu || scratch[current].other != 0u { fail(h, 2u); return; }
        scratch[current].other = 1u;
        scratch[base + i].next = current;
        let y = (scratch[current].a.xyz - origin) - compensation;
        let next_sum = sum + y; compensation = (next_sum - sum) - y; sum = next_sum;
        current = point_lookup(points, scratch[current].b.xyz);
    }
    if current != first { fail(h, 2u); return; }
    let center = origin + sum / f32(boundary_count);
    let outward = p.plane.xyz * select(-1.0, 1.0, h == 1u);
    for (var i = 0u; i < boundary_count; i++) {
        let e = scratch[scratch[base + i].next];
        let area = dot(cross(e.b.xyz - e.a.xyz, center - e.a.xyz), outward);
        if !(area > 0.00000001) { fail(h, 5u); return; }
    }
    if atomicLoad(&metadata[h].count) + boundary_count * 3u > p.counts.y { fail(h, 3u); return; }
    for (var i = 0u; i < boundary_count; i++) {
        let e = scratch[scratch[base + i].next];
        let normal = vec4<f32>(outward, 0.0);
        triangle(h, Vertex(vec4<f32>(center, 1.0), normal, p.color),
            Vertex(vec4<f32>(e.a.xyz, 1.0), normal, p.color),
            Vertex(vec4<f32>(e.b.xyz, 1.0), normal, p.color));
    }
}

var<workgroup> measure_lo: array<vec3<f32>, 64>;
var<workgroup> measure_hi: array<vec3<f32>, 64>;
var<workgroup> measure_volume: array<f32, 64>;
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn measure(@builtin(workgroup_id) group: vec3<u32>, @builtin(local_invocation_index) lane: u32) {
    let h = group.x;
    let count = min(atomicLoad(&metadata[h].count), p.counts.y); let base = h * p.counts.y;
    var lo = vec3<f32>(BIG); var hi = vec3<f32>(-BIG);
    for (var i = lane; i < count; i += 64u) {
        let v = output[base + i].position.xyz;
        if !finite(v) { fail(h, 4u); }
        lo = min(lo, v); hi = max(hi, v);
    }
    measure_lo[lane] = lo; measure_hi[lane] = hi; workgroupBarrier();
    for (var stride = 32u; stride > 0u; stride /= 2u) {
        if lane < stride {
            measure_lo[lane] = min(measure_lo[lane], measure_lo[lane + stride]);
            measure_hi[lane] = max(measure_hi[lane], measure_hi[lane + stride]);
        }
        workgroupBarrier();
    }
    lo = measure_lo[0]; hi = measure_hi[0];
    var sum = 0.0; var compensation = 0.0;
    for (var i = lane * 3u; i < count; i += 64u * 3u) {
        let a = output[base + i].position.xyz - lo;
        let b = output[base + i + 1u].position.xyz - lo;
        let c = output[base + i + 2u].position.xyz - lo;
        let value = dot(a, cross(b, c)) / 6.0;
        let y = value - compensation; let t = sum + y;
        compensation = (t - sum) - y; sum = t;
    }
    measure_volume[lane] = sum; workgroupBarrier();
    for (var stride = 32u; stride > 0u; stride /= 2u) {
        if lane < stride { measure_volume[lane] += measure_volume[lane + stride]; }
        workgroupBarrier();
    }
    if lane == 0u {
        sum = measure_volume[0];
        metadata[h].lo = vec4<f32>(lo, 0.0); metadata[h].hi = vec4<f32>(hi, 0.0);
        metadata[h].volume = vec4<f32>(sum, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
        if !(sum > 0.0 && sum <= BIG) && atomicLoad(&metadata[h].status) == 0u { fail(h, 4u); }
    }
}

var<workgroup> reduction: array<f32, 64>;
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn support(@builtin(workgroup_id) group: vec3<u32>, @builtin(local_invocation_index) lane: u32) {
    let h = group.x / 64u; let sample = group.x % 64u;
    if p.a.w == 1.0 && sample != 0u { return; }
    var value = BIG;
    let count = min(atomicLoad(&metadata[h].count), p.counts.y);
    if atomicLoad(&metadata[h].status) == 0u {
        for (var i = lane; i < count; i += 64u) {
            value = min(value, dot(p.directions[group.x].xyz, output[h * p.counts.y + i].position.xyz));
        }
    }
    reduction[lane] = value; workgroupBarrier();
    for (var stride = 32u; stride > 0u; stride /= 2u) {
        if lane < stride { reduction[lane] = min(reduction[lane], reduction[lane + stride]); }
        workgroupBarrier();
    }
    if lane == 0u {
        if p.a.w == 1.0 {
            for (var i = 0u; i < 64u; i++) { metadata[h].samples[i] = reduction[0]; }
        } else { metadata[h].samples[sample] = reduction[0]; }
    }
}

@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn pack(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid: vec3<u32>) {
    let h = gid.y; let i = gid.x;
    if atomicLoad(&metadata[h].status) != 0u || i >= min(atomicLoad(&metadata[h].count), p.counts.y) { return; }
    let v = output[h * p.counts.y + i];
    let base = h * p.counts.y * 10u;
    let pos = bitcast<vec3<u32>>(v.position.xyz); let color = bitcast<vec3<u32>>(v.color.xyz);
    let normal = bitcast<vec3<u32>>(v.normal.xyz);
    for (var k = 0u; k < 3u; k++) {
        packed[base + i * 9u + k] = pos[k];
        packed[base + i * 9u + 3u + k] = color[k];
        packed[base + i * 9u + 6u + k] = normal[k];
    }
    packed[base + p.counts.y * 9u + i] = i;
}


# File: src/render/data.rs
use wgpu::VertexBufferLayout;
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
pub struct Vertex {
    pub position: [f32; 3],
    pub color: [f32; 3],
    pub normal: [f32; 3],
}

impl Vertex {
    pub fn layout<'a>() -> VertexBufferLayout<'a> {
        use std::mem;
        VertexBufferLayout {
            array_stride: mem::size_of::<Vertex>() as wgpu::BufferAddress,
            step_mode: wgpu::VertexStepMode::Vertex,
            attributes: &[
                wgpu::VertexAttribute {
                    offset: 0,
                    shader_location: 0,
                    format: wgpu::VertexFormat::Float32x3,
                },
                wgpu::VertexAttribute {
                    offset: mem::size_of::<[f32; 3]>() as wgpu::BufferAddress,
                    shader_location: 1,
                    format: wgpu::VertexFormat::Float32x3,
                },
                wgpu::VertexAttribute {
                    offset: (mem::size_of::<[f32; 3]>() * 2) as wgpu::BufferAddress,
                    shader_location: 2,
                    format: wgpu::VertexFormat::Float32x3,
                },
            ],
        }
    }
}

pub const VERTICES: &[Vertex] = &[
    // front - red
    Vertex {
        position: [-0.5, -0.5, 0.5],
        color: [1.0, 0.0, 0.0],
        normal: [0.0, 0.0, 1.0],
    },
    Vertex {
        position: [0.5, -0.5, 0.5],
        color: [1.0, 0.0, 0.0],
        normal: [0.0, 0.0, 1.0],
    },
    Vertex {
        position: [0.5, 0.5, 0.5],
        color: [1.0, 0.0, 0.0],
        normal: [0.0, 0.0, 1.0],
    },
    Vertex {
        position: [-0.5, 0.5, 0.5],
        color: [1.0, 0.0, 0.0],
        normal: [0.0, 0.0, 1.0],
    },
    // back - green
    Vertex {
        position: [0.5, -0.5, -0.5],
        color: [0.0, 1.0, 0.0],
        normal: [0.0, 0.0, -1.0],
    },
    Vertex {
        position: [-0.5, -0.5, -0.5],
        color: [0.0, 1.0, 0.0],
        normal: [0.0, 0.0, -1.0],
    },
    Vertex {
        position: [-0.5, 0.5, -0.5],
        color: [0.0, 1.0, 0.0],
        normal: [0.0, 0.0, -1.0],
    },
    Vertex {
        position: [0.5, 0.5, -0.5],
        color: [0.0, 1.0, 0.0],
        normal: [0.0, 0.0, -1.0],
    },
    // left - blue
    Vertex {

    // right - yellow
