                "FRUSTUM {}/{} CULLED {}",
                q.visible,
                q.total,
                q.total.saturating_sub(q.visible)
            ),
            format!(
                "BVH {} NODES TESTS {}",
                q.culling_stats.nodes_visited, q.culling_stats.object_tests
            ),
            format!("DRAWS {} MAIN {}", self.draw_calls, self.main_draw_calls),
        ]
    }

    pub fn set_effects(&mut self, shadows: bool, reflections: bool) {
        let effects = self.effects.as_mut().unwrap();
        if effects.shadows != shadows {
            effects.reflection_key = None;
        }
        effects.shadows = shadows;
        effects.reflections = reflections;
    }
    pub fn effects_stats(&self) -> [f64; 19] {
        let e = self.effects.as_ref().unwrap();
        let s = &e.stats;
        [
            s.static_updates as f64,
            s.dynamic_updates as f64,
            s.reflection_updates as f64,
            s.static_visible as f64,
            s.dynamic_visible as f64,
            s.reflection_visible as f64,
            s.passes as f64,
            e.static_shadow[0].size as f64,
            e.reflection.width as f64,
            e.reflection.height as f64,
            s.shadow_light_count as f64,
            s.static_updates_by_light[0] as f64,
            s.static_updates_by_light[1] as f64,
            s.static_updates_by_light[2] as f64,
            s.static_updates_by_light[3] as f64,
            s.dynamic_updates_by_light[0] as f64,
            s.dynamic_updates_by_light[1] as f64,
            s.dynamic_updates_by_light[2] as f64,
            s.dynamic_updates_by_light[3] as f64,
        ]
    }
    /// Auxiliary views have independent culling/buffer caches; simulation advances separately.
    /// Submit each pass before preparing another view, preserving shared uniform write ordering.
    pub fn render_view(
        &mut self,
        view: RenderView,
        target: &RenderTarget<'_>,
    ) -> Result<(), String> {
        target.validate()?;
        let mut effects = self.effects.take().unwrap();
        let result = effects.render(self, view, target);
        self.effects = Some(effects);
        result
    }
    /// Submit a culled depth-only view without updating simulation or running color effects.
    pub fn render_depth_view(
        &mut self,
        view: RenderView,
        target: &DepthRenderTarget<'_>,
        pass: RenderPassKind,
    ) -> Result<(), String> {
        target.validate()?;
        self.prepare_pass(view, pass);
        self.draw_depth(target)
    }
    pub(super) fn draw_depth(&mut self, target: &DepthRenderTarget<'_>) -> Result<(), String> {
        target.validate()?;
        self.shadow_pipelines
            .entry(target.format)
            .or_insert_with(|| {
                pipeline::build_shadow(&self.device, &self.bind_group_layout, target.format)
            });
        let gltf_key = super::gltf_gpu::PipelineKey {
            format: wgpu::TextureFormat::Rgba8Unorm,
            depth_format: target.format,
            reflected: false,
            material_mask: false,
            shadow: true,
        };
        for id in self.passes[self.active_cache].queue.imported.keys() {
            if let Some(model) = self.gltf_models.get(id) {
                self.gltf_gpu
                    .prepare_pipeline(&self.device, gltf_key, model);
            }
        }
        let pipeline = &self.shadow_pipelines[&target.format];
        let cache = &self.passes[self.active_cache];
        let mut draws = 0;
        let mut encoder = self
            .device
            .create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor {
                label: Some("shadow encoder"),
            });
        {
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor {
                label: Some("shadow casters"),
                color_attachments: &[],
                depth_stencil_attachment: Some(wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment {
                    view: target.depth,
                    depth_ops: Some(wgpu::Operations {
                        load: target.load,
                        store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
                    }),
                    stencil_ops: None,
                }),
                timestamp_writes: None,
                occlusion_query_set: None,
                multiview_mask: None,
            });
            pass.set_pipeline(pipeline);
            pass.set_viewport(
                target.viewport.x as f32,
                target.viewport.y as f32,
                target.viewport.width as f32,
                target.viewport.height as f32,
                0.0,
                1.0,
            );
            pass.set_scissor_rect(
                target.viewport.x,
                target.viewport.y,
                target.viewport.width,
                target.viewport.height,
            );
            pass.set_bind_group(0, &self.grid_bind_group, &[]);
            for (index, kind) in [MeshKind::Cube, MeshKind::Plane, MeshKind::Sphere]
                .into_iter()
                .enumerate()
            {
                let mesh = self.mesh(kind);
                for batch in [&cache.batches[index], &cache.mirrored_batches[index]] {
                    let count = batch.uploaded.len() as u32;
                    if count == 0 {
                        continue;
                    }
                    pass.set_vertex_buffer(0, mesh.vertex_buffer.slice(..));
                    pass.set_vertex_buffer(1, batch.buffer.slice(..));
                    pass.set_index_buffer(mesh.index_buffer.slice(..), mesh.index_format);
                    pass.draw_indexed(0..mesh.index_count, 0, 0..count);
                    draws += 1;
                }
            }
            for (id, instances) in &cache.queue.imported {
                if instances.is_empty() {
                    continue;
                }
                let Some(mesh) = self.imported_meshes.get(id) else {
                    continue;
                };
                pass.set_vertex_buffer(0, mesh.vertex_buffer.slice(..));
                pass.set_vertex_buffer(1, cache.imported[id].buffer.slice(..));
                pass.set_index_buffer(mesh.index_buffer.slice(..), mesh.index_format);
                pass.draw_indexed(0..mesh.index_count, 0, 0..instances.len() as u32);
                draws += 1;
            }
            for id in cache.queue.imported.keys() {
                if let Some(model) = self.gltf_models.get(id) {
                    draws += self.gltf_gpu.draw(
                        &mut pass,
                        model,
                        gltf_key,
                        false,
                        cache.camera_position,
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        self.queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
        self.draw_calls += draws;
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Draw developer overlays after postprocessing; exclude them from reflection/depth inputs.
    pub fn draw_helpers(&mut self, target: &RenderTarget<'_>) {
        if !self.draw_grid && !self.draw_light_helpers {
            return;
        }
        let key = (target.color_format, target.depth_format);
        self.helper_pipelines.entry(key).or_insert_with(|| {
            pipeline::build_lines(&self.device, key.0, key.1, &self.bind_group_layout)
        });
        let mut encoder = self
            .device
            .create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor {
                label: Some("postprocess helpers"),
            });
        {
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor {
                label: Some("developer overlays"),
                color_attachments: &[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment {
                    view: target.color,
                    resolve_target: None,
                    depth_slice: None,
                    ops: wgpu::Operations {
                        load: wgpu::LoadOp::Load,
                        store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
                    },
                })],
                depth_stencil_attachment: Some(wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment {
                    view: target.depth,
                    depth_ops: Some(wgpu::Operations {
                        load: wgpu::LoadOp::Load,
                        store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
                    }),
                    stencil_ops: None,
                }),
                timestamp_writes: None,
                occlusion_query_set: None,
                multiview_mask: None,
            });
            pass.set_pipeline(&self.helper_pipelines[&key]);
            pass.set_bind_group(0, &self.grid_bind_group, &[]);
            pass.set_viewport(
                target.viewport.x as f32,
                target.viewport.y as f32,
                target.viewport.width as f32,
                target.viewport.height as f32,
                0.0,
                1.0,
            );
            if self.draw_grid {
                pass.set_vertex_buffer(0, self.grid_vertex_buffer.slice(..));
                pass.draw(0..self.grid_vertex_count, 0..1);
                self.draw_calls += 1;
                self.main_draw_calls += 1;
            }
            if self.draw_light_helpers {
                pass.set_vertex_buffer(0, self.light_vertex_buffer.slice(..));
                pass.draw(0..self.light_vertex_count, 0..1);
                self.draw_calls += 1;
                self.main_draw_calls += 1;
            }
        }
        self.queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
    }
    pub(super) fn draw_color(
        &mut self,
        target: &RenderTarget<'_>,
        effects: &Effects,
        reflected: bool,
    ) -> Result<(), String> {
        target.validate()?;
        let mut draws = 0;
        let key = (
            target.color_format,
            target.depth_format,
            self.passes[self.active_cache]
                .key
                .is_some_and(|k| k.0.determinant() < 0.0),
            target.auxiliary.is_some(),
        );
        self.pipelines.entry(key).or_insert_with(|| {
            (
                pipeline::build(
                    &self.device,
                    key.0,
                    key.1,
                    &self.bind_group_layout,
                    &effects.layout,
                    key.2,
                    key.3,
                ),
                pipeline::build_lines(&self.device, key.0, key.1, &self.bind_group_layout),
                pipeline::build(
                    &self.device,
                    key.0,
                    key.1,
                    &self.bind_group_layout,
                    &effects.layout,
                    !key.2,
                    key.3,
                ),
            )
        });
        let gltf_key = super::gltf_gpu::PipelineKey {
            format: target.color_format,
            depth_format: target.depth_format,
            reflected: self.passes[self.active_cache]
                .key
                .is_some_and(|k| k.0.determinant() < 0.0),
            material_mask: target.auxiliary.is_some(),
            shadow: false,
        };
        for id in self.passes[self.active_cache].queue.imported.keys() {
            if let Some(model) = self.gltf_models.get(id) {
                self.gltf_gpu
                    .prepare_pipeline(&self.device, gltf_key, model);
            }
        }
        // Immutable lookup ends the map mutation before borrowing mesh resources.
        let pipelines = &self.pipelines[&key];
        let cache = &self.passes[self.active_cache];
        let mut encoder = self
            .device
            .create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor {
                label: Some("encoder"),
            });
        let mut attachments = vec![Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment {
            view: target.color,
            resolve_target: None,
            depth_slice: None,
            ops: wgpu::Operations {
                load: target.color_load,
                store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
            },
        })];
        if let Some(auxiliary) = target.auxiliary {
            attachments.push(Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment {
                view: auxiliary,
                resolve_target: None,
                depth_slice: None,
                ops: wgpu::Operations {
                    load: match target.depth_load {
                        wgpu::LoadOp::Load => wgpu::LoadOp::Load,
                        wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(_) => wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(wgpu::Color::WHITE),
                        wgpu::LoadOp::DontCare(v) => wgpu::LoadOp::DontCare(v),
                    },
                    store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
                },
            }));
        }
        {
            let mut rp = encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor {
                label: Some("render"),
                color_attachments: &attachments,
                depth_stencil_attachment: Some(wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment {
                    view: target.depth,
                    depth_ops: Some(wgpu::Operations {
                        load: target.depth_load,
                        store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
                    }),
                    stencil_ops: None,
                }),
                occlusion_query_set: None,
                timestamp_writes: None,
                multiview_mask: None,
            });
            rp.set_viewport(
                target.viewport.x as f32,
                target.viewport.y as f32,
                target.viewport.width as f32,
                target.viewport.height as f32,
                0.0,
                1.0,
            );
            rp.set_scissor_rect(
                target.viewport.x,
                target.viewport.y,
                target.viewport.width,
                target.viewport.height,
            );
            rp.set_pipeline(&pipelines.0);
            rp.set_bind_group(
                1,
                if reflected {
                    &effects.reflection_group
                } else {
                    &effects.scene_group
                },
                &[],
            );
            rp.set_bind_group(0, &self.grid_bind_group, &[]);
            for (index, kind) in [MeshKind::Cube, MeshKind::Plane, MeshKind::Sphere]
                .into_iter()
                .enumerate()
            {
                let mesh = self.mesh(kind);
                for (batch, pipeline) in [
                    (&cache.batches[index], &pipelines.0),
                    (&cache.mirrored_batches[index], &pipelines.2),
                ] {
                    let count = batch.uploaded.len() as u32;
                    if count == 0 {
                        continue;
                    }
                    rp.set_pipeline(pipeline);
                    rp.set_vertex_buffer(0, mesh.vertex_buffer.slice(..));
                    rp.set_vertex_buffer(1, batch.buffer.slice(..));
                    rp.set_index_buffer(mesh.index_buffer.slice(..), mesh.index_format);
                    rp.draw_indexed(0..mesh.index_count, 0, 0..count);
                    draws += 1;
                }
            }
            for (id, instances) in &cache.queue.imported {
                if instances.is_empty() {
                    continue;
                }
                let Some(mesh) = self.imported_meshes.get(id) else {
                    continue;
                };
                rp.set_pipeline(
                    if Mat4::from_cols_array_2d(&instances[0].model).determinant() < 0.0 {
                        &pipelines.2
                    } else {
                        &pipelines.0
                    },
                );
                rp.set_vertex_buffer(0, mesh.vertex_buffer.slice(..));
                rp.set_vertex_buffer(1, cache.imported[id].buffer.slice(..));
                rp.set_index_buffer(mesh.index_buffer.slice(..), mesh.index_format);
                rp.draw_indexed(0..mesh.index_count, 0, 0..instances.len() as u32);
                draws += 1;
            }
            for id in cache.queue.imported.keys() {
                if let Some(model) = self.gltf_models.get(id) {
                    draws +=
                        self.gltf_gpu
                            .draw(&mut rp, model, gltf_key, false, cache.camera_position);
                }
            }
            if reflected || target.auxiliary.is_none() {
                draws += self.gltf_gpu.draw_many(
                    &mut rp,
                    cache
                        .queue
                        .imported
                        .keys()
                        .filter_map(|id| self.gltf_models.get(id)),
                    gltf_key,
                    true,
                    cache.camera_position,
                );
            }
            if !reflected
                && target.auxiliary.is_none()
                && (self.draw_grid || self.draw_light_helpers)
            {
                rp.set_pipeline(&pipelines.1);
                rp.set_bind_group(0, &self.grid_bind_group, &[]);
                if target.auxiliary.is_none() && self.draw_grid {
                    rp.set_vertex_buffer(0, self.grid_vertex_buffer.slice(..));
                    rp.draw(0..self.grid_vertex_count, 0..1);
                    draws += 1;
                }
                if target.auxiliary.is_none() && self.draw_light_helpers {
                    rp.set_vertex_buffer(0, self.light_vertex_buffer.slice(..));
                    rp.draw(0..self.light_vertex_count, 0..1);
                    draws += 1;
                }
            }
        }
        self.queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
        self.draw_calls += draws;
        if !reflected {
            self.main_draw_calls += draws;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Transparent materials use opaque depth after screen-space effects.
    pub fn draw_transparent(&mut self, target: &RenderTarget<'_>) -> Result<(), String> {
        let cache = &self.passes[0];
        if !cache
            .queue
            .imported
            .keys()
            .filter_map(|id| self.gltf_models.get(id))
            .any(|m| super::gltf_gpu::draw_count(m, true) > 0)
        {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let key = super::gltf_gpu::PipelineKey {
            format: target.color_format,
            depth_format: target.depth_format,
            reflected: cache.key.is_some_and(|k| k.0.determinant() < 0.0),
            material_mask: false,
            shadow: false,
        };
        for id in cache.queue.imported.keys() {
            if let Some(model) = self.gltf_models.get(id) {
                self.gltf_gpu.prepare_pipeline(&self.device, key, model);
            }
        }
        let effects = self.effects.as_ref().unwrap();
        let mut encoder = self
            .device
            .create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor {
                label: Some("glTF transparent overlay"),
            });
        {
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor {
                label: Some("glTF transparent overlay"),
                color_attachments: &[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment {
                    view: target.color,
                    resolve_target: None,
                    depth_slice: None,
                    ops: wgpu::Operations {
                        load: wgpu::LoadOp::Load,
                        store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
                    },
                })],
                depth_stencil_attachment: Some(wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment {
                    view: target.depth,
                    depth_ops: Some(wgpu::Operations {
                        load: wgpu::LoadOp::Load,
                        store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
                    }),
                    stencil_ops: None,
                }),
                timestamp_writes: None,
                occlusion_query_set: None,
                multiview_mask: None,
            });
            pass.set_viewport(
                target.viewport.x as f32,
                target.viewport.y as f32,
                target.viewport.width as f32,
                target.viewport.height as f32,
                0.0,
                1.0,
            );
            pass.set_scissor_rect(
                target.viewport.x,
                target.viewport.y,
                target.viewport.width,
                target.viewport.height,
            );
            pass.set_bind_group(0, &self.grid_bind_group, &[]);
            pass.set_bind_group(1, &effects.scene_group, &[]);
            let draws = self.gltf_gpu.draw_many(
                &mut pass,
                cache
                    .queue
                    .imported
                    .keys()
                    .filter_map(|id| self.gltf_models.get(id)),
                key,
                true,
                cache.camera_position,
            );
            self.draw_calls += draws;
            self.main_draw_calls += draws;
        }
        self.queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
        Ok(())
    }
}


# File: src/render/shadow.wgsl
@group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> view_projection: mat4x4<f32>;
struct Input {
    @location(0) position:vec3<f32>,
    @location(3) model0:vec4<f32>, @location(4) model1:vec4<f32>,
    @location(5) model2:vec4<f32>, @location(6) model3:vec4<f32>,
};
@vertex fn vs_main(input:Input)->@builtin(position) vec4<f32> {
    let model=mat4x4<f32>(input.model0,input.model1,input.model2,input.model3);
    return view_projection*model*vec4(input.position,1.0);
}


# File: src/render/state.rs
//! Browser host connecting the scene renderer to a canvas output.
use super::{
    output::CanvasOutput, postprocess::PostProcessor, renderer::SceneRenderer, target::RenderView,
};
use glam::{Mat4, Vec3};
use std::ops::{Deref, DerefMut};
use wasm_bindgen::JsValue;
use web_sys::HtmlCanvasElement;

pub struct State {
    renderer: SceneRenderer,
    output: CanvasOutput,
    postprocess: PostProcessor,
    pub backend: String,
    pub aspect: f32,
    view: RenderView,
    model_camera: Option<(u32, usize)>,
    debug_enabled: bool,
    debug_hud: Option<super::hud::DebugHud>,
    frame_dt: f32,
    cutting_compute: Option<super::cutting_compute::ComputeCutter>,
    resident_cutter: Option<super::cutting_resident::ResidentCutter>,
    resident_sources:
        std::collections::BTreeMap<u32, std::rc::Rc<super::cutting_resident::ResidentSource>>,
    pub cutting_busy: std::rc::Rc<std::cell::Cell<bool>>,
}
impl Deref for State {
    type Target = SceneRenderer;
    fn deref(&self) -> &Self::Target {
        &self.renderer
    }
}
impl DerefMut for State {
    fn deref_mut(&mut self) -> &mut Self::Target {
        &mut self.renderer
    }
}
impl State {
    pub async fn new(
        canvas: &HtmlCanvasElement,
        check_current: impl Fn() -> Result<(), JsValue>,
    ) -> Result<Self, JsValue> {
        let (output, device, queue, backend) = CanvasOutput::new(canvas, &check_current).await?;
        check_current()?;
        let aspect = output.aspect();
        let renderer = SceneRenderer::new(device, queue);
        let mut postprocess = PostProcessor::new(&renderer.device, output.color_format());
        let (static_map, dynamic_map) = renderer.shadow_maps();
        postprocess.set_shadow_maps(&renderer.device, static_map, dynamic_map);
        Ok(Self {
            renderer,
            output,
            postprocess,
            backend,
            aspect,
            model_camera: None,
            debug_enabled: false,
            debug_hud: None,
            frame_dt: 0.0,
            cutting_compute: None,
            resident_cutter: None,
            resident_sources: Default::default(),
            cutting_busy: Default::default(),
            view: RenderView {
                view_projection: Mat4::IDENTITY,
                camera_position: Vec3::ZERO,
            },
        })
    }
    pub fn resize(&mut self, width: u32, height: u32) {
        self.output.resize(&self.renderer.device, width, height);
        self.aspect = self.output.aspect();
    }
    pub fn dispatch_cut_geometry(
        &mut self,
        inputs: &[crate::cutting::CutGeometryInput],
    ) -> super::cutting_compute::PendingCutGeometry {
        let renderer = &self.renderer;
        self.cutting_compute
            .get_or_insert_with(|| super::cutting_compute::ComputeCutter::new(&renderer.device))
            .dispatch(&renderer.device, &renderer.queue, inputs)
    }
    pub fn resident_devices(&self) -> (wgpu::Device, wgpu::Queue) {
        (self.renderer.device.clone(), self.renderer.queue.clone())
    }
    pub fn dispatch_resident(
        &mut self,
        candidate: &crate::scene::ResidentCandidate,
    ) -> Result<super::cutting_resident::PendingResidentCut, String> {
        use super::cutting_resident::*;
        use wgpu::util::DeviceExt;
        let renderer = &self.renderer;
        if let std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Vacant(entry) =
            self.resident_sources.entry(candidate.id)
        {
            let mesh = candidate.mesh.cpu()?;
            if !mesh.resident_eligible() {
                return Err("Resident cutting requires one connected genus-zero solid".into());
            }
            let vertices: Vec<_> = mesh
                .render_mesh()
                .vertices
                .iter()
                .map(|v| ResidentVertex {
                    position: [v.position[0], v.position[1], v.position[2], 0.],
                    normal: [v.normal[0], v.normal[1], v.normal[2], 0.],
                    color: [v.color[0], v.color[1], v.color[2], 0.],
                })
                .collect();
            let buffer = renderer
                .device
                .create_buffer_init(&wgpu::util::BufferInitDescriptor {
                    label: Some("resident original soup"),
                    contents: bytemuck::cast_slice(&vertices),
                    usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_SRC,
                });
            entry.insert(std::rc::Rc::new(ResidentSource {
                vertices: buffer,
                vertex_count: vertices.len() as u32,
            }));
        }
        let params = ResidentCutParams {
            a: candidate.a,
            b: candidate.b,
            motion: candidate.motion,
            cap_color: candidate.color,
            source_volume: candidate.mesh.volume() as f32,
            support_dirs: candidate.support_dirs(),
        };
        self.resident_cutter
            .get_or_insert_with(|| ResidentCutter::new(&renderer.device))
            .dispatch(
                &renderer.device,
                &renderer.queue,
                &self.resident_sources[&candidate.id],
                &params,
            )
    }
    pub fn install_resident(&mut self, id: u32, piece: super::cutting_resident::ResidentPiece) {
        let source = self.renderer.install_resident(id, piece);
        self.resident_sources.insert(id, std::rc::Rc::new(source));
    }
    pub fn resident_source(
        &self,
        id: u32,
    ) -> Option<std::rc::Rc<super::cutting_resident::ResidentSource>> {
        self.resident_sources.get(&id).cloned()
    }
    pub fn configure_god_rays(&mut self, density: f32, exposure: f32) -> bool {
        self.postprocess.configure_volume(density, exposure)
    }
    pub fn set_postprocess(&mut self, name: &str, enabled: bool, strength: f32) -> bool {
        self.postprocess.set_effect(name, enabled, strength)
    }
    pub fn set_reflection_mask(
        &mut self,
        width: u32,
        height: u32,
        pixels: &[u8],
        center: glam::Vec2,
        size: glam::Vec2,
    ) -> bool {
        self.postprocess.set_reflection_mask(
            &self.renderer.device,
            &self.renderer.queue,
            width,
            height,
            pixels,
            center,
            size,
        )
    }
    pub fn postprocess_stats(&self) -> [f64; 5] {
        self.postprocess.stats()
    }
    pub fn set_output_mode(&mut self, mode: &str) -> bool {
        match mode {
            "canvas" => self.output.offscreen = false,
            "texture" => self.output.offscreen = true,
            _ => return false,
        }
        true
    }
    pub fn update(&mut self, dt: f32, camera_matrix: Mat4, camera_pos: Vec3) {
        self.frame_dt = dt;
        self.renderer.advance_scene(dt);
        self.view = RenderView {
            view_projection: camera_matrix,
            camera_position: camera_pos,
        };
        if let Some((id, node)) = self.model_camera {
            if let Some(view) = self.imported_camera_view(id, node) {
                self.view = view;
            } else {
                self.model_camera = None;
            }
        }
    }
    pub fn clear_model_camera(&mut self) {
        self.model_camera = None;
    }
    pub fn screen_to_plane(&self, u: f32, v: f32, height: f32) -> Option<Vec3> {
        crate::render_math::screen_to_horizontal_plane(self.view.view_projection, u, v, height)
    }
    pub fn world_to_screen(&self, point: Vec3) -> Option<[f32; 2]> {
        let p = self.view.view_projection * point.extend(1.0);
        if !p.is_finite() || p.w <= 0.0 {
            return None;
        }
        let p = p.truncate() / p.w;
        (p.x.abs() <= 1.0 && p.y.abs() <= 1.0 && (0.0..=1.0).contains(&p.z))
            .then_some([(p.x + 1.0) * 0.5, (1.0 - p.y) * 0.5])
    }
    pub fn use_model_camera(&mut self, id: u32, node: usize) -> bool {
        if self.imported_camera_view(id, node).is_none() {
            return false;
        }
        self.model_camera = Some((id, node));
        true
    }
    fn imported_camera_view(&self, id: u32, node: usize) -> Option<RenderView> {
        let model = self.scene.model(id)?;
        if !model.pose.active_nodes.contains(&node) {
            return None;
        }
        let camera = model.asset.nodes.get(node)?.camera?;
        let root = self.scene.objects().get(&id)?.transform.model();
        let world = root * model.pose.world_matrices[node];
        if world.determinant().abs() < 1e-10 {
            return None;
        }
        let projection = match &model.asset.cameras[camera] {
            crate::gltf_model::ModelCamera::Perspective {
                yfov,
                aspect_ratio,
                znear,
                zfar,
                ..
            } => match zfar {
                Some(far) => glam::camera::rh::proj::directx::perspective(
                    *yfov,
                    aspect_ratio.unwrap_or(self.aspect),
                    *znear,
                    *far,
                ),
                None => glam::camera::rh::proj::directx::perspective_infinite(
                    *yfov,
                    aspect_ratio.unwrap_or(self.aspect),
                    *znear,
                ),
            },
            crate::gltf_model::ModelCamera::Orthographic {
                xmag,
                ymag,
                znear,
                zfar,
                ..
            } => glam::camera::rh::proj::directx::orthographic(
                -*xmag, *xmag, -*ymag, *ymag, *znear, *zfar,
            ),
        };
        Some(RenderView {
            view_projection: projection * world.inverse(),
            camera_position: world.transform_point3(Vec3::ZERO),
        })
    }
    pub fn set_debug_overlay(&mut self, enabled: bool) {
        self.debug_enabled = enabled;
        self.renderer.set_debug_diagnostics(enabled);
        if enabled {
            if let Some(hud) = &mut self.debug_hud {
                hud.invalidate();
            }
        }
    }
    pub fn debug_stats(&self) -> String {
        let mut value = self.renderer.debug_values();
        value["enabled"] = self.debug_enabled.into();
        value["hudFrames"] = self.debug_hud.as_ref().map_or(0, |h| h.frames).into();
        value["hudRefreshes"] = self.debug_hud.as_ref().map_or(0, |h| h.refreshes).into();
        value.to_string()
    }
    pub fn render(&mut self) -> Result<(), JsValue> {
        let Some(frame) = self.output.acquire(&self.renderer.device)? else {
            return Ok(());
        };
        self.output.ensure_target(&self.renderer.device);
        if let Some(compute) = &mut self.cutting_compute {
            compute.retain(|id| self.renderer.scene.triangle_meshes().contains_key(&id));
        }
        self.resident_sources
            .retain(|id, _| self.renderer.scene.object_exists(*id));
        self.renderer.draw_calls = 0;
        self.renderer.main_draw_calls = 0;
        let target = self.output.target(&frame);
        if self.postprocess.enabled() {
            self.postprocess
                .ensure_size(&self.renderer.device, target.width, target.height);
            self.renderer
                .render_view(self.view, &self.postprocess.target())
                .map_err(|e| JsValue::from_str(&e))?;
            // Effects lazily replaces fallback maps when shadows are first enabled.
            // Rebind by resource identity; unchanged maps do not allocate a new bind group.
            let (static_map, dynamic_map) = self.renderer.shadow_maps();
            self.postprocess
                .set_shadow_maps(&self.renderer.device, static_map, dynamic_map);
            let light = self.renderer.scene.lights().values().next();
            let (position, color) = light
                .map(|l| (l.position, l.color))
                .unwrap_or((Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ZERO));
            self.renderer.draw_calls += self.postprocess.render(
                &self.renderer.device,
                &self.renderer.queue,
                self.view,
                position,
                color,
                target.color,
                self.renderer.shadows_enabled(),
            );
            let overlays = super::target::RenderTarget {
                depth: self.postprocess.target().depth,
                auxiliary: None,
                ..target
            };
            self.renderer
                .draw_transparent(&overlays)
                .map_err(|e| JsValue::from_str(&e))?;
            self.renderer.draw_helpers(&overlays);
        } else {
            self.renderer
                .render_view(self.view, &target)
                .map_err(|e| JsValue::from_str(&e))?;
        }
        // Include the imminent HUD and final texture blit in the displayed total.
        self.renderer.draw_calls +=
            usize::from(self.debug_enabled) + usize::from(self.output.offscreen);
        if self.debug_enabled {
            let renderer = &self.renderer;
            self.renderer.upload_bytes +=
                self.debug_hud.get_or_insert_with(Default::default).render(
                    &renderer.device,
                    &renderer.queue,
                    &target,
                    self.frame_dt,
                    || renderer.debug_lines(),
                );
        }
        self.output
            .present(frame, &self.renderer.device, &self.renderer.queue);
        Ok(())
    }
}


# File: src/render/target.rs
//! Render inputs independent of any browser canvas or surface.
use glam::{Mat4, Vec3};

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub struct RenderView {
    pub view_projection: Mat4,
    pub camera_position: Vec3,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Viewport {
    pub x: u32,
    pub y: u32,
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
}
impl Viewport {
    pub fn full(width: u32, height: u32) -> Self {
        Self {
            x: 0,
            y: 0,
            width,
            height,
        }
    }
    pub fn validate(self, width: u32, height: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
        if self.width == 0 || self.height == 0 || width == 0 || height == 0 {
            return Err("Render target and viewport must have nonzero dimensions".into());
        }
        if self
            .x
            .checked_add(self.width)
            .is_none_or(|right| right > width)
            || self
                .y
                .checked_add(self.height)
                .is_none_or(|bottom| bottom > height)
        {
            return Err("Viewport must fit inside the render target".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Single-sample color and depth attachments. Their formats and dimensions
/// must match this descriptor; wgpu validates the underlying GPU resources.
pub struct RenderTarget<'a> {
    pub color: &'a wgpu::TextureView,
    /// Optional packed depth attachment for portable screen-space effects.
    pub auxiliary: Option<&'a wgpu::TextureView>,
    pub depth: &'a wgpu::TextureView,
    pub color_format: wgpu::TextureFormat,
    pub depth_format: wgpu::TextureFormat,
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    pub viewport: Viewport,
    /// Use Load for additional views sharing an attachment; Clear clears the whole attachment.
    pub color_load: wgpu::LoadOp<wgpu::Color>,
    pub depth_load: wgpu::LoadOp<f32>,
}
impl RenderTarget<'_> {
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.viewport.validate(self.width, self.height)
    }
}

/// A reusable depth-only pass target, e.g. a shadow map or depth prepass.
pub struct DepthRenderTarget<'a> {
    pub depth: &'a wgpu::TextureView,
    pub format: wgpu::TextureFormat,
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    pub viewport: Viewport,
    pub load: wgpu::LoadOp<f32>,
}
impl DepthRenderTarget<'_> {
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.viewport.validate(self.width, self.height)?;
        if !self.format.has_depth_aspect() {
            return Err("Depth target requires a depth format".into());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn viewport_accepts_full_target_and_subrect() {
        assert!(Viewport::full(800, 600).validate(800, 600).is_ok());
        assert!(Viewport {
            x: 400,
            y: 100,
            width: 400,
            height: 500
        }
        .validate(800, 600)
        .is_ok());
    }
    #[test]
    fn viewport_rejects_empty_outside_and_overflow() {
        assert!(Viewport::full(0, 600).validate(800, 600).is_err());
        assert!(Viewport::full(800, 600).validate(0, 600).is_err());
        assert!(Viewport {
            x: 401,
            y: 0,
            width: 400,
            height: 600
        }
        .validate(800, 600)
        .is_err());
        assert!(Viewport {
            x: u32::MAX,
            y: 0,
            width: 1,
            height: 1
        }
        .validate(u32::MAX, 600)
        .is_err());
    }
}


# File: src/render/textures.rs
//! Owned, reusable single-sample render attachments, independent of presentation.
use super::target::{DepthRenderTarget, RenderTarget, Viewport};
pub struct DepthTarget {
    pub texture: wgpu::Texture,
    pub view: wgpu::TextureView,
    pub size: u32,
}
impl DepthTarget {
    pub const FORMAT: wgpu::TextureFormat = wgpu::TextureFormat::Depth32Float;
    pub fn target(&self) -> DepthRenderTarget<'_> {
        DepthRenderTarget {
            depth: &self.view,
            format: Self::FORMAT,
            width: self.size,
            height: self.size,
            viewport: Viewport::full(self.size, self.size),
            load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(1.0),
        }
    }
    pub fn new(device: &wgpu::Device, label: &str, size: u32) -> Self {
        let texture = device.create_texture(&wgpu::TextureDescriptor {
            label: Some(label),
            size: wgpu::Extent3d {
                width: size,
                height: size,
                depth_or_array_layers: 1,
            },
            mip_level_count: 1,
            sample_count: 1,
            dimension: wgpu::TextureDimension::D2,
            format: Self::FORMAT,
            usage: wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT | wgpu::TextureUsages::TEXTURE_BINDING,
            view_formats: &[],
        });
        let view = texture.create_view(&Default::default());
        Self {
            texture,
            view,
            size,
        }
    }
}
pub struct ColorTarget {
    pub texture: wgpu::Texture,
    pub color: wgpu::TextureView,
    pub depth_texture: wgpu::Texture,
    pub depth: wgpu::TextureView,
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    pub format: wgpu::TextureFormat,
}
impl ColorTarget {
    pub fn new(
        device: &wgpu::Device,
        label: &str,
        width: u32,
        height: u32,
        format: wgpu::TextureFormat,
    ) -> Self {
        assert!(width > 0 && height > 0);
        let texture = device.create_texture(&wgpu::TextureDescriptor {
            label: Some(label),
            size: wgpu::Extent3d {
                width,
                height,
                depth_or_array_layers: 1,
            },
            mip_level_count: 1,
            sample_count: 1,
            dimension: wgpu::TextureDimension::D2,
            format,
            usage: wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT | wgpu::TextureUsages::TEXTURE_BINDING,
            view_formats: &[],
        });
        let color = texture.create_view(&Default::default());
        let (depth_texture, depth) =
            super::depth::create(device, width, height, DepthTarget::FORMAT);
        Self {
            texture,
            color,
            depth_texture,
            depth,
            width,
            height,
            format,
        }
    }
    pub fn target(&self) -> RenderTarget<'_> {
        RenderTarget {
            color: &self.color,
            depth: &self.depth,
            auxiliary: None,
            color_format: self.format,
            depth_format: DepthTarget::FORMAT,
            width: self.width,
            height: self.height,
            viewport: Viewport::full(self.width, self.height),
            color_load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(wgpu::Color {
                r: 0.1,
                g: 0.1,
                b: 0.3,
                a: 1.0,
            }),
            depth_load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(1.0),
        }
    }
}


# File: src/render_math.rs
//! View math shared by shadow/mirror passes and native tests.
use glam::{Mat4, Vec3, Vec4};

/// Normalized DOM coordinates (left/top=0) projected onto world horizontal plane.
pub fn screen_to_horizontal_plane(matrix: Mat4, u: f32, v: f32, height: f32) -> Option<Vec3> {
    if !u.is_finite()
        || !v.is_finite()
        || !height.is_finite()
        || !(0.0..=1.0).contains(&u)
        || !(0.0..=1.0).contains(&v)
    {
        return None;
    }
    let inverse = matrix.inverse();
    if !inverse.is_finite() {
        return None;
    }
    let near = inverse.project_point3(Vec3::new(u * 2.0 - 1.0, 1.0 - v * 2.0, 0.0));
    let far = inverse.project_point3(Vec3::new(u * 2.0 - 1.0, 1.0 - v * 2.0, 1.0));
    let direction = far - near;
    if !near.is_finite() || !direction.is_finite() || direction.y.abs() < 1e-6 {
        return None;
    }
    let t = (height - near.y) / direction.y;
    let point = near + direction * t;
    (t >= 0.0 && point.is_finite()).then_some(point)
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct MirrorPlane {
    pub normal: Vec3,
    pub distance: f32,
}
impl MirrorPlane {
    pub fn new(normal: Vec3, distance: f32) -> Option<Self> {
        let length = normal.length();
        if !normal.is_finite() || !distance.is_finite() || length < 1e-6 {
            return None;
        }
        Some(Self {
            normal: normal / length,
            distance: distance / length,
        })
    }
    pub fn matrix(self) -> Mat4 {
        let n = self.normal;
        Mat4::from_cols(
            (Vec3::X - 2.0 * n.x * n).extend(0.0),
            (Vec3::Y - 2.0 * n.y * n).extend(0.0),
            (Vec3::Z - 2.0 * n.z * n).extend(0.0),
            (-2.0 * self.distance * n).extend(1.0),
        )
    }
    pub fn clip_plane(self, camera: Vec3) -> Vec4 {
        let plane = self.normal.extend(self.distance);
        if self.normal.dot(camera) + self.distance < 0.0 {
            -plane
        } else {
            plane
        }
    }
}
pub fn shadow_view_projection(position: Vec3) -> Mat4 {
    let center = Vec3::new(0.0, -0.5, 0.0);
    let target = if center.distance_squared(position) < 1e-8 {
        position + Vec3::NEG_Z
    } else {
        center
    };
    let direction = (target - position).normalize_or_zero();
    let up = if direction.dot(Vec3::Y).abs() > 0.99 {
        Vec3::Z
    } else {
        Vec3::Y
    };
    glam::camera::lh::proj::directx::perspective(1.5, 1.0, 0.1, 30.0)
        * glam::camera::lh::view::look_at_mat4(position, target, up)
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn pointer_projection_matches_visible_camera_and_rejects_invalid_rays() {
        let mut camera = crate::input::camera::Camera::new(1.5);
        assert!(camera.set_view(Vec3::new(5., 6., 7.), Vec3::new(0., 1., 0.)));
        let matrix = camera.matrix();
        let p = screen_to_horizontal_plane(matrix, 0.5, 0.5, 1.0).unwrap();
        assert!(p.length().is_finite());
        assert!(p.distance(Vec3::Y) < 1e-3);
        let visible = matrix.project_point3(p);
        assert!(visible.x.abs() < 1e-4 && visible.y.abs() < 1e-4);
        assert!(screen_to_horizontal_plane(matrix, -0.1, 0.5, 0.).is_none());
        assert!(screen_to_horizontal_plane(Mat4::ZERO, 0.5, 0.5, 0.).is_none());
        assert!(screen_to_horizontal_plane(Mat4::IDENTITY, 0.5, 0.5, 0.).is_none());
    }
    #[test]
    fn mirror_reflects_camera_and_preserves_surface_points() {
        let p = MirrorPlane::new(Vec3::Z * 2.0, 6.0).unwrap();
        let r = p.matrix();
        assert!(
            (r.transform_point3(Vec3::new(1.0, 2.0, 5.0)) - Vec3::new(1.0, 2.0, -11.0)).length()
                < 1e-5
        );
        let surface = Vec3::new(1.0, 2.0, -3.0);
        assert!((r.transform_point3(surface) - surface).length() < 1e-5);
        assert!((r * r - Mat4::IDENTITY)
            .to_cols_array()
            .iter()
            .all(|v| v.abs() < 1e-5));
        assert!(r.determinant() < 0.0);
        assert!(p.clip_plane(Vec3::ZERO).dot(Vec3::ZERO.extend(1.0)) > 0.0);
        assert!(MirrorPlane::new(Vec3::ZERO, 0.0).is_none());
    }
    #[test]
    fn light_frustum_keeps_off_camera_shadow_casters() {
        let light = Vec3::new(-3.0, 7.0, 4.0);
        let f = crate::visibility::Frustum::from_view_projection(shadow_view_projection(light));
        assert!(f.intersects_aabb(Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::splat(0.5)));
        assert!(!f.intersects_aabb(light * 2.0, Vec3::splat(0.1)));
    }
}


# File: src/scene.rs
//! CPU scene state and simulation. No browser, JavaScript or GPU dependencies.
use glam::{Mat4, Vec3};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub const MAX_LIGHTS: usize = 4;
pub const INVALID_ID: u32 = u32::MAX;

pub struct TriangleObject {
    pub mesh: crate::cutting_mesh::MeshData,
    pub cuttable: bool,
    pub cap_color: Vec3,
}
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct CutReport {
    pub removed: Vec<u32>,
    pub pieces: Vec<u32>,
    pub object_candidates: usize,
    pub bvh_nodes: usize,
    pub triangle_tests: usize,
    piece_directions: Vec<Vec3>,
}
#[derive(PartialEq)]
pub struct CutSnapshot {
    cutter: (u32, [Vec3; 2], Mat4),
    targets: Vec<(u32, Mat4, Vec3)>,
}
/// Owned immutable inputs; safe to retain while the GPU request is pending.
pub struct ResidentCandidate {
    pub id: u32,
    pub mesh: crate::cutting_mesh::MeshData,
    pub a: Vec3,
    pub b: Vec3,
    pub motion: Vec3,
    pub color: Vec3,
    pub transform: Transform,
    pub options: RenderOptions,
    pub direction: Vec3,
    pub angular: [Vec3; 2],
}
impl ResidentCandidate {
    pub fn support_dirs(&self) -> [[f32; 4]; 128] {
        std::array::from_fn(|i| {
            let matrix = Transform::new(
                Vec3::ZERO,
                self.transform.rotation + self.angular[i / 64] * ((i % 64) as f32 / 63.),
                self.transform.scale,
            )
            .model();
            matrix
                .transpose()
                .transform_vector3(Vec3::Y)
                .extend(0.)
                .to_array()
        })
    }
}

struct CutScatter {
    start: Transform,
    pivot: Vec3,
    direction: Vec3,
    angular: Vec3,
    floor_y: f32,
    distance: f32,
    duration: f32,
    elapsed: f32,
}

/// World-space normals must remain perpendicular to transformed tangents.
pub(crate) fn normal_matrix(model: Mat4) -> Mat4 {
    model.inverse().transpose()
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum MeshKind {
    Cube,
    Plane,
    Sphere,
    Imported(u32),
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Transform {
    pub position: Vec3,
    pub rotation: Vec3,
    pub scale: Vec3,
}

impl Transform {
    pub fn new(position: Vec3, rotation: Vec3, scale: Vec3) -> Self {
        Self {
            position,
            rotation,
            scale,
        }
    }
    pub fn model(&self) -> Mat4 {
        Mat4::from_translation(self.position)
            * Mat4::from_rotation_y(self.rotation.y)
            * Mat4::from_rotation_x(self.rotation.x)
            * Mat4::from_rotation_z(self.rotation.z)
            * Mat4::from_scale(self.scale)
    }
    fn is_valid(&self) -> bool {
        self.position.is_finite()
            && self.rotation.is_finite()
            && self.scale.is_finite()
            && self.scale.abs().min_element() > 0.0
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct RenderOptions {
    pub is_static: bool,
    pub casts_shadow: bool,
    pub receives_shadow: bool,
    pub reflectivity: f32,
}
impl Default for RenderOptions {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            is_static: false,
            casts_shadow: true,
            receives_shadow: true,
            reflectivity: 0.0,
        }
    }
}

pub struct SceneObject {
    pub transform: Transform,
    pub mesh: MeshKind,
    pub render_options: RenderOptions,
    local_bounds: Option<(Vec3, Vec3)>,
    pub(crate) render_data: crate::visibility::ObjectRenderData,
}
impl SceneObject {
    fn refresh_render_data(&mut self) {
        self.render_data = if let Some((min, max)) = self.local_bounds {
            crate::visibility::ObjectRenderData::with_bounds(self.transform, min, max)
        } else {
            crate::visibility::ObjectRenderData::new(self.mesh, self.transform)
        };
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub struct Light {
    pub position: Vec3,
    pub color: Vec3,
}
struct RotationAction {
    id: u32,
    start: f32,
    duration: f32,
    delta: Vec3,
    elapsed: f32,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
enum RemovalKind {
    Object,
    Light,
}
struct ScheduledRemoval {
    id: u32,
    at: f32,
    kind: RemovalKind,
}
struct LightOrbit {
    radius: f32,
    height: f32,
    speed: f32,
    phase: f32,
    pulse: f32,
    color: Vec3,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
struct BoxCollider {
    half_extents: Vec3,
    offset: Vec3,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct SceneManager {
    objects: BTreeMap<u32, SceneObject>,
    triangle_meshes: BTreeMap<u32, TriangleObject>,
    cutters: BTreeMap<u32, [Vec3; 2]>,
    cutting_index: crate::spatial::Bvh,
    cutting_swings: BTreeMap<u32, (Vec3, Vec3, f32)>,
    cutting_scatter: BTreeMap<u32, CutScatter>,
    // Current preview: cutter, first dashed-line handle, plane/origin/finite axis.
    cutting_guide: Option<(u32, u32, [[f32; 4]; 3])>,
    models: BTreeMap<u32, crate::gltf_model::ModelInstance>,
    lights: BTreeMap<u32, Light>,
    light_orbits: BTreeMap<u32, LightOrbit>,
    next_id: u32,
    render_revision: u64,
    static_revision: u64,
    spins: BTreeMap<u32, Vec3>,
    projectiles: BTreeMap<u32, (Vec3, f32, f32)>,
    projectile_scratch: Vec<crate::projectile::Body>,
    projectile_order: Vec<usize>,
    projectile_pairs: Vec<(usize, usize)>,
    pub projectile_contacts: u32,
    pub paused: bool,
    pub mirror_object: Option<u32>,
    time: f32,
    rotations: Vec<RotationAction>,
    removals: Vec<ScheduledRemoval>,
    colliders: BTreeMap<u32, BoxCollider>,
    collision_world: crate::collision::CollisionWorld,
    collision_dirty: bool,
    visibility_index: std::cell::RefCell<crate::visibility::VisibilityIndex>,
}

impl SceneManager {
    pub(crate) fn visibility_index(
        &self,
        collect_stats: bool,
    ) -> std::cell::RefMut<'_, crate::visibility::VisibilityIndex> {
        let mut index = self.visibility_index.borrow_mut();
        index.sync(self, collect_stats);
        index
    }
    pub fn configure_mirror(&mut self, id: u32) -> bool {
        if !self
            .objects
            .get(&id)
            .is_some_and(|o| o.mesh == MeshKind::Plane && o.render_options.reflectivity > 0.0)
        {
            return false;
        }
        self.mirror_object = Some(id);
        self.changed(id);
        true
    }
    pub fn mirror_plane(&self) -> Option<crate::render_math::MirrorPlane> {
        let object = self.mirror_object.and_then(|id| self.objects.get(&id))?;
        if object.render_options.reflectivity <= 0.0 {
            return None;
        }
        let normal = object.render_data.plane_normal();
        crate::render_math::MirrorPlane::new(normal, -normal.dot(object.transform.position))
    }
    pub fn mirror_visible(&self, matrix: Mat4, culling: bool) -> bool {
        let Some(object) = self.mirror_object.and_then(|id| self.objects.get(&id)) else {
            return false;
        };
        !culling
            || object
                .render_data
                .intersects(&crate::visibility::Frustum::from_view_projection(matrix))
    }
    pub fn static_revision(&self) -> u64 {
        self.static_revision
    }
    fn changed(&mut self, id: u32) {
        if self.colliders.contains_key(&id) {
            self.collision_dirty = true;
        }
        self.render_revision = self.render_revision.wrapping_add(1);
        if self
            .objects
            .get(&id)
            .is_some_and(|o| o.render_options.is_static)
        {
            self.static_revision = self.static_revision.wrapping_add(1);
        }
    }
    pub fn set_render_options(&mut self, id: u32, options: RenderOptions) -> bool {
        if !options.reflectivity.is_finite() || !(0.0..=1.0).contains(&options.reflectivity) {
            return false;
        }
        let Some(object) = self.objects.get_mut(&id) else {
            return false;
        };
        let was_static = object.render_options.is_static;
        object.render_options = options;
        self.changed(id);
        if was_static && !options.is_static {
            self.static_revision = self.static_revision.wrapping_add(1);
        }
        true
    }

    /// Explicit local-space box, independent of render geometry or animation bounds.
    pub fn set_box_collider(&mut self, id: u32, half_extents: Vec3, offset: Vec3) -> bool {
        if !self.object_exists(id)
            || !half_extents.is_finite()
            || half_extents.min_element() <= 0.0
            || !offset.is_finite()
        {
            return false;
        }
        self.colliders.insert(
            id,
            BoxCollider {
                half_extents,
                offset,
            },
        );
        self.collision_dirty = true;
        true
    }

    pub fn remove_collider(&mut self, id: u32) -> bool {
        if !self.object_exists(id) {
            return false;
        }
        self.collision_dirty |= self.colliders.remove(&id).is_some();
        true
    }

    /// Cached BVH; movement of a non-solid character never rebuilds the world.
    pub fn collision_world(&mut self) -> &crate::collision::CollisionWorld {
        if self.collision_dirty {
            let colliders = self
                .colliders
                .iter()
                .filter_map(|(id, collider)| {
                    let transform = self.objects.get(id)?.transform;
                    let model = transform.model();
                    let center = model.transform_point3(collider.offset);
                    // Rotated authored boxes use conservative world-aligned bounds.
                    let half = model.x_axis.truncate().abs() * collider.half_extents.x
                        + model.y_axis.truncate().abs() * collider.half_extents.y
                        + model.z_axis.truncate().abs() * collider.half_extents.z;
                    crate::collision::Aabb::new(center - half, center + half)
                        .map(|bounds| crate::collision::Collider { id: *id, bounds })
                })
                .collect();
            self.collision_world.rebuild(colliders);
            self.collision_dirty = false;
        }
        &self.collision_world
    }
    pub fn set_object_spin(&mut self, id: u32, radians_per_second: Vec3) -> bool {
        if !self.object_exists(id) || !radians_per_second.is_finite() {
            return false;
        }
        if radians_per_second == Vec3::ZERO {
            self.spins.remove(&id);
        } else {
            self.spins.insert(id, radians_per_second);
        }
        true
    }
    pub fn set_projectile(&mut self, id: u32, velocity: Vec3, gravity: f32, bounce: f32) -> bool {
        if !self.object_exists(id)
            || !velocity.is_finite()
            || !gravity.is_finite()
            || !bounce.is_finite()
            || gravity < 0.0
            || !(0.0..=1.0).contains(&bounce)
        {
            return false;
        }
        if gravity == 0.0 && velocity == Vec3::ZERO {
            self.projectiles.remove(&id);
        } else {
            self.projectiles.insert(id, (velocity, gravity, bounce));
        }
        true
    }
    fn update_projectiles(&mut self, dt: f32) {
        if self.projectiles.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let mut bodies = std::mem::take(&mut self.projectile_scratch);
        bodies.clear();
        for (&id, &(velocity, gravity, bounce)) in &self.projectiles {
            if let Some(object) = self.objects.get(&id) {
                bodies.push(crate::projectile::Body {
                    id,
                    velocity,
                    gravity,
                    bounce,
                    position: object.transform.position,
                    radius: object.transform.scale.abs().max_element() * 1.15,
                });
            }
        }
        let contacts = crate::projectile::simulate(
            &mut bodies,
            &mut self.projectile_order,
            &mut self.projectile_pairs,
            dt,
        );
        self.projectile_contacts = self.projectile_contacts.saturating_add(contacts);
        for body in &bodies {
            self.projectiles.get_mut(&body.id).unwrap().0 = body.velocity;
            let object = self.objects.get_mut(&body.id).unwrap();
            object.transform.position = body.position;
            object.refresh_render_data();
            self.collision_dirty |= self.colliders.contains_key(&body.id);
            self.render_revision = self.render_revision.wrapping_add(1);
            if object.render_options.is_static {
                self.static_revision = self.static_revision.wrapping_add(1);
            }
        }
        self.projectile_scratch = bodies;
    }
    pub fn render_revision(&self) -> u64 {
        self.render_revision
    }

    // IDs are never reused, including after clear/reset. Old handles stay invalid.
    fn allocate_id(&mut self) -> Option<u32> {
        if self.next_id >= i32::MAX as u32 {
            return None;
        }
        let id = self.next_id;
        self.next_id += 1;
        Some(id)
    }
    pub fn objects(&self) -> &BTreeMap<u32, SceneObject> {
        &self.objects
    }
    pub fn lights(&self) -> &BTreeMap<u32, Light> {
        &self.lights
    }
    pub fn models(&self) -> &BTreeMap<u32, crate::gltf_model::ModelInstance> {
        &self.models
    }
    pub fn model(&self, id: u32) -> Option<&crate::gltf_model::ModelInstance> {
        self.models.get(&id)
    }
    pub fn add_model(
        &mut self,
        asset: std::sync::Arc<crate::gltf_model::ModelAsset>,
    ) -> Result<u32, String> {
        let model = crate::gltf_model::ModelInstance::new(asset, None)?;
        let (min, max) = crate::gltf_animation::model_bounds(&model.asset, &model.pose)?;
        let id = self.add_imported(min, max);
        if id == INVALID_ID {
            return Err("Cannot allocate model root".into());
        }
        self.models.insert(id, model);
        Ok(id)
    }
    fn commit_model(
        &mut self,
        id: u32,
        model: crate::gltf_model::ModelInstance,
    ) -> Result<(), String> {
        let (min, max) = crate::gltf_animation::model_bounds(&model.asset, &model.pose)?;
        let object = self
            .objects
            .get_mut(&id)
            .ok_or("Model root does not exist")?;
        object.local_bounds = Some((min, max));
        object.refresh_render_data();
        self.models.insert(id, model);
        self.changed(id);
        Ok(())
    }
    fn mutate_model(
        &mut self,
        id: u32,
        operation: impl FnOnce(&mut crate::gltf_model::ModelInstance) -> Result<(), String>,
    ) -> Result<(), String> {
        let mut candidate = self
            .models
            .get(&id)
            .ok_or("Model root does not exist")?
            .clone();
        operation(&mut candidate)?;
        candidate.last_error = None;
        self.commit_model(id, candidate)
    }
    pub fn play_model_animation(
        &mut self,
        id: u32,
        clip: usize,
        looping: bool,
        speed: f32,
    ) -> Result<(), String> {
        if !speed.is_finite() {
            return Err("Animation speed must be finite".into());
        }
        self.mutate_model(id, |model| {
            model.play(clip, looping)?;
            model.playback.as_mut().unwrap().speed = speed;
            Ok(())
        })
    }
    pub fn pause_model_animation(&mut self, id: u32, paused: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
        let model = self
            .models
            .get_mut(&id)
            .ok_or("Model root does not exist")?;
        model
            .playback
            .as_mut()
            .ok_or("Model has no active animation")?
            .playing = !paused;
        model.playback_revision = model.playback_revision.wrapping_add(1);
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn seek_model_animation(&mut self, id: u32, time: f32) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.mutate_model(id, |model| model.seek(time))
    }
    pub fn stop_model_animation(&mut self, id: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.mutate_model(id, |model| model.stop())
    }
    pub fn select_model_scene(&mut self, id: u32, scene: usize) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.mutate_model(id, |model| model.select_scene(scene))
    }
    pub fn set_model_morph_weights(
        &mut self,
        id: u32,
        node: usize,
        weights: &[f32],
    ) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.mutate_model(id, |model| model.set_morph_weights(node, weights))
    }
    pub fn object_exists(&self, id: u32) -> bool {
        self.objects.contains_key(&id)
    }
    pub fn light_exists(&self, id: u32) -> bool {
        self.lights.contains_key(&id)
    }
    pub fn add_object(&mut self, mesh: MeshKind, transform: Transform) -> u32 {
        if !transform.is_valid() {
            return INVALID_ID;
        }
        let Some(id) = self.allocate_id() else {
            return INVALID_ID;
        };
        self.objects.insert(
            id,
            SceneObject {
                mesh,
                transform,
                render_options: RenderOptions::default(),
                local_bounds: None,
                render_data: crate::visibility::ObjectRenderData::new(mesh, transform),
            },
        );
        self.render_revision = self.render_revision.wrapping_add(1);
        id
    }
    pub fn add_imported(&mut self, min: Vec3, max: Vec3) -> u32 {
        if !min.is_finite() || !max.is_finite() || min.cmpgt(max).any() {
            return INVALID_ID;
        }
        let id = self.add_object(
            MeshKind::Imported(0),
            Transform::new(Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ONE),
        );
        if let Some(object) = self.objects.get_mut(&id) {
            object.mesh = MeshKind::Imported(id);
            object.local_bounds = Some((min, max));
            object.refresh_render_data();
        }
        id
    }
    pub fn triangle_meshes(&self) -> &BTreeMap<u32, TriangleObject> {
        &self.triangle_meshes
    }
    pub fn add_triangle_mesh(&mut self, mesh: crate::cutting::CutMesh) -> Result<u32, String> {
        self.add_mesh_data(mesh.into())
    }
    fn add_mesh_data(&mut self, mesh: crate::cutting_mesh::MeshData) -> Result<u32, String> {
        let total: usize = self
            .triangle_meshes
            .values()
            .map(|o| o.mesh.triangle_count())
            .sum();
        if total + mesh.triangle_count() > 200_000 {
            return Err("Scene cutting triangle budget exceeded".into());
        }
        let (min, max) = mesh.bounds();
        let id = self.add_imported(min, max);
        if id == INVALID_ID {
            return Err("Object IDs exhausted".into());
        }
        self.triangle_meshes.insert(
            id,
            TriangleObject {
                mesh,
                cuttable: false,
                cap_color: Vec3::new(1., 0.3, 0.12),
            },
        );
        Ok(id)
    }
    pub fn set_cuttable(&mut self, id: u32, enabled: bool, cap_color: Vec3) -> Result<(), String> {
        if !cap_color.is_finite() || cap_color.min_element() < 0. || cap_color.max_element() > 1. {
            return Err("Invalid cap color".into());
        }
        if enabled && self.cutters.contains_key(&id) {
            return Err("Cutter cannot also be cuttable".into());
        }
        let object = self
            .triangle_meshes
            .get_mut(&id)
            .ok_or("Cuttable requires a custom triangle mesh")?;
        object.cuttable = enabled;
        object.cap_color = cap_color;
        self.render_revision = self.render_revision.wrapping_add(1);
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn is_cuttable(&self, id: u32) -> bool {
        self.triangle_meshes.get(&id).is_some_and(|o| o.cuttable)
    }
    pub fn cutting_guide_uniforms(&self) -> [[f32; 4]; 3] {
        self.cutting_guide
            .filter(|(cutter, guide, _)| {
                self.cutters.contains_key(cutter)
                    && self
                        .objects
                        .get(guide)
                        .is_some_and(|o| o.transform.scale.abs().min_element() > 0.0001)
            })
            .map_or([[0.0; 4]; 3], |(_, _, uniforms)| uniforms)
    }
    pub fn aim_cutter(
        &mut self,
        id: u32,
        guides: &[u32],
        aim: crate::cutting_tool::ToolAim,
    ) -> Result<Vec3, String> {
        let blade = *self
            .cutters
            .get(&id)
            .ok_or("Object is not a registered cutter")?;
        let object = self.objects.get(&id).ok_or("Cutter no longer exists")?;
        if guides.len() > 64
            || guides.iter().any(|&guide| {
                guide == id
                    || !self.object_exists(guide)
                    || self.triangle_meshes.get(&guide).is_some_and(|o| o.cuttable)
            })
        {
            return Err("Invalid cutting guide objects".into());
        }
        let pose = aim.pose(blade, object.transform.scale)?;
        let model = Transform::new(pose.position, pose.rotation, object.transform.scale).model();
        let middle = model.transform_point3((blade[0] + blade[1]) * 0.5);
        let normal = pose.direction.cross(pose.motion).normalize();
        self.cutting_guide = guides.first().map(|&guide| {
            (
                id,
                guide,
                [
                    normal.extend(-normal.dot(middle)).to_array(),
                    middle.extend(aim.floor).to_array(),
                    pose.direction.extend(pose.length * 0.5).to_array(),
                ],
            )
        });
        self.set_object_position(id, pose.position);
        self.set_object_rotation(id, pose.rotation);
        let spacing = pose.length / (guides.len().max(1) as f32);
        for (i, &guide) in guides.iter().enumerate() {
            let along = (i as f32 + 0.5) * spacing - pose.length * 0.5;
            self.set_object_transform(
                guide,
                pose.guide_center + pose.direction * along,
                pose.rotation,
                Vec3::new(0.035, 0.008, spacing * 0.32),
            );
        }
        Ok(pose.motion)
    }
    pub fn set_cutter(&mut self, id: u32, blade: Option<[Vec3; 2]>) -> Result<(), String> {
        if !self.object_exists(id) {
            return Err("Cutter object does not exist".into());
        }
        if let Some([a, b]) = blade {
            if !a.is_finite()
                || !b.is_finite()
                || a.abs().max_element() > 10_000.
                || b.abs().max_element() > 10_000.
                || a.distance(b) < 1e-4
            {
                return Err("Invalid local blade endpoints".into());
            }
            if self.triangle_meshes.get(&id).is_some_and(|o| o.cuttable) {
                return Err("Cuttable object cannot also be cutter".into());
            }
            self.cutters.insert(id, [a, b]);
        } else {
            self.cutters.remove(&id);
        }
        self.render_revision = self.render_revision.wrapping_add(1);
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Explicit straight sweep command. Geometry and new object ownership stay in Rust.
    /// All cuts are prepared first: a topology error never removes any source object.
    pub fn cut_with(
        &mut self,
        cutter: u32,
        motion: Vec3,
        separation: f32,
    ) -> Result<CutReport, String> {
        self.cut_with_geometry(cutter, motion, separation, None)
    }
    pub fn cut_geometry_inputs(
        &self,
        cutter: u32,
        motion: Vec3,
    ) -> Result<Vec<crate::cutting::CutGeometryInput>, String> {
        if !motion.is_finite() || motion.abs().max_element() > 1000. {
            return Err("Invalid cutting sweep".into());
        }
        let blade = self
            .cutters
            .get(&cutter)
            .ok_or("Object is not a registered cutter")?;
        let model = self
            .objects
            .get(&cutter)
            .ok_or("Cutter no longer exists")?
            .transform
            .model();
        let [a, b] = blade.map(|p| model.transform_point3(p));
        let normal = (b - a).cross(motion).normalize_or_zero();
        if normal.length_squared() < 0.5 {
            return Err("Invalid cutting sweep".into());
        }
        let min = a.min(b).min(a + motion).min(b + motion) - Vec3::splat(1e-4);
        let max = a.max(b).max(a + motion).max(b + motion) + Vec3::splat(1e-4);
        let mut inputs = Vec::new();
        for (&id, source) in &self.triangle_meshes {
            if !source.cuttable {
                continue;
            }
            let model = self.objects[&id].transform.model();
            let (lo, hi) = source.mesh.bounds();
            let center = model.transform_point3((lo + hi) * 0.5);
            let half = (hi - lo) * 0.5;
            let radius = model.x_axis.truncate().abs() * half.x
                + model.y_axis.truncate().abs() * half.y
                + model.z_axis.truncate().abs() * half.z;
            if (center + radius).cmpge(min).all()
                && (center - radius).cmple(max).all()
                && normal.dot(center - a).abs() <= normal.abs().dot(radius) + 1e-4
            {
                let inverse = model.inverse();
                if !inverse.is_finite() {
                    return Err("Noninvertible cutting transform".into());
                }
                inputs.push(source.mesh.cpu()?.geometry_input(
                    id,
                    inverse.transform_point3(a),
                    inverse.transform_point3(b),
                    inverse.transform_vector3(motion),
                )?);
            }
        }
        Ok(inputs)
    }
    pub fn cut_snapshot(&self, cutter: u32) -> Result<CutSnapshot, String> {
        let blade = *self
            .cutters
            .get(&cutter)
            .ok_or("Cutter no longer registered")?;
        let model = self
            .objects
            .get(&cutter)
            .ok_or("Cutter no longer exists")?
            .transform
            .model();
        Ok(CutSnapshot {
            cutter: (cutter, blade, model),
            targets: self
                .triangle_meshes
                .iter()
                .filter(|(_, o)| o.cuttable)
                .map(|(&id, o)| (id, self.objects[&id].transform.model(), o.cap_color))
                .collect(),
        })
    }
    pub fn resident_candidates(
        &self,
        cutter: u32,
        motion: Vec3,
        separation: f32,
        scatter: Option<[f32; 3]>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ResidentCandidate>, String> {
        if !separation.is_finite()
            || !(0.0..=2.0).contains(&separation)
            || !motion.is_finite()
            || motion.abs().max_element() > 1000.
        {
            return Err("Invalid cutting sweep".into());
        }
        let blade = self.cutters.get(&cutter).ok_or("Object is not a cutter")?;
        let matrix = self
            .objects
            .get(&cutter)
            .ok_or("Cutter no longer exists")?
            .transform
            .model();
        let [a, b] = blade.map(|p| matrix.transform_point3(p));
        let normal = (b - a).cross(motion).normalize_or_zero();
        if normal.length_squared() < 0.5 {
            return Err("Cut motion must cross blade".into());
        }
        if let Some([floor, distance, duration]) = scatter {
            if !floor.is_finite()
                || floor.abs() > 10_000.
                || !distance.is_finite()
                || !(0.0..=3.0).contains(&distance)
                || !duration.is_finite()
                || !(0.05..=3.0).contains(&duration)
                || Vec3::new(normal.x, 0., normal.z).length_squared() < 0.5e-6
            {
                return Err("Invalid fragment animation options".into());
            }
        }
        let min = a.min(b).min(a + motion).min(b + motion) - Vec3::splat(1e-4);
        let max = a.max(b).max(a + motion).max(b + motion) + Vec3::splat(1e-4);
        let mut candidates = Vec::new();
        for (&id, source) in &self.triangle_meshes {
            if !source.cuttable {
                continue;
            }
            let object = &self.objects[&id];
            let model = object.transform.model();
            let (lo, hi) = source.mesh.bounds();
            let center = model.transform_point3((lo + hi) * 0.5);
            let half = (hi - lo) * 0.5;
            let radius = model.x_axis.truncate().abs() * half.x
                + model.y_axis.truncate().abs() * half.y
                + model.z_axis.truncate().abs() * half.z;
            if (center + radius).cmpge(min).all()
                && (center - radius).cmple(max).all()
                && normal.dot(center - a).abs() <= normal.abs().dot(radius) + 1e-4
            {
                let inverse = model.inverse();
                if !inverse.is_finite() {
                    return Err("Noninvertible cutting transform".into());
                }
                let direction = normal * model.determinant().signum();
                let angular = std::array::from_fn(|side| {
                    if scatter.is_none() {
                        return Vec3::ZERO;
                    }
                    let dir = if side == 0 { direction } else { -direction };
                    let dir = Vec3::new(dir.x, 0., dir.z).normalize();
                    let sign = if dir.x + dir.z >= 0. { 1. } else { -1. };
                    Vec3::new(dir.z * 1.15, sign * 0.12, -dir.x * 1.15)
                });
                candidates.push(ResidentCandidate {
                    id,
                    mesh: source.mesh.clone(),
                    a: inverse.transform_point3(a),
                    b: inverse.transform_point3(b),
                    motion: inverse.transform_vector3(motion),
                    color: source.cap_color,
                    transform: object.transform,
                    options: object.render_options,
                    direction,
                    angular,
                });
            }
        }
        Ok(candidates)
    }
    /// Atomic scene replacement after every GPU result has been validated.
    pub fn commit_resident(
        &mut self,
        cutter: u32,
        motion: Vec3,
        separation: f32,
        scatter: Option<[f32; 3]>,
        mut pending: Vec<(ResidentCandidate, [crate::cutting_mesh::MeshData; 2])>,
        candidate_count: usize,
    ) -> Result<CutReport, String> {
        let removed: Vec<_> = pending.iter().map(|(c, _)| c.id).collect();
        let retained: usize = self
            .triangle_meshes
            .iter()
            .filter(|(id, _)| !removed.contains(id))
            .map(|(_, o)| o.mesh.triangle_count())
            .sum();
        let total: usize = pending
            .iter()
            .flat_map(|(_, pieces)| pieces)
            .map(|m| m.triangle_count())
            .sum();
        if retained + total > 200_000 {
            return Err("Scene cutting triangle budget exceeded".into());
        }
        if self
            .next_id
            .checked_add((pending.len() * 2) as u32)
            .is_none_or(|id| id == INVALID_ID)
        {
            return Err("Object IDs exhausted".into());
        }
        let cutter_start = self
            .objects
            .get(&cutter)
            .ok_or("Cutter no longer exists")?
            .transform
            .position;
        for (candidate, _) in &mut pending {
            candidate.options = self
                .objects
                .get(&candidate.id)
                .ok_or("Cut target no longer exists")?
                .render_options;
        }
        for &id in &removed {
            self.remove_object(id);
        }
        let mut report = CutReport {
            removed,
            object_candidates: candidate_count,
            ..Default::default()
        };
        for (candidate, pieces) in pending {
            for (side, piece) in pieces.into_iter().enumerate() {
                let direction = if side == 0 {
                    candidate.direction
                } else {
                    -candidate.direction
                };
                let mut transform = candidate.transform;
                transform.position += direction * separation * 0.5;
                let id = self.add_mesh_data(piece)?;
                self.set_object_transform(
                    id,
                    transform.position,
                    transform.rotation,
                    transform.scale,
                );
                self.set_render_options(id, candidate.options);
                self.set_cuttable(id, true, candidate.color)?;
                report.pieces.push(id);
                report.piece_directions.push(direction);
            }
        }
        if let Some([floor, distance, duration]) = scatter {
            self.scatter_cut_pieces(
                &report.pieces,
                &report.piece_directions,
                floor,
                distance,
                duration,
            );
        }
        self.cutting_swings
            .insert(cutter, (cutter_start, cutter_start + motion, 0.));
        Ok(report)
    }
    pub fn cut_with_geometry(
        &mut self,
        cutter: u32,
        motion: Vec3,
