        separation: f32,
        geometry: Option<&BTreeMap<u32, crate::cutting::CutGeometry>>,
    ) -> Result<CutReport, String> {
        if !motion.is_finite()
            || motion.abs().max_element() > 1000.
            || !separation.is_finite()
            || !(0.0..=2.0).contains(&separation)
        {
            return Err("Invalid cutting sweep".into());
        }
        let blade = *self
            .cutters
            .get(&cutter)
            .ok_or("Object is not a registered cutter")?;
        let transform = self
            .objects
            .get(&cutter)
            .ok_or("Cutter no longer exists")?
            .transform;
        let [a, b] = blade.map(|p| transform.model().transform_point3(p));
        let normal = (b - a).cross(motion).normalize_or_zero();
        if normal.length_squared() < 0.5 {
            return Err("Cut motion must cross the blade direction".into());
        }
        let min = a.min(b).min(a + motion).min(b + motion) - Vec3::splat(1e-4);
        let max = a.max(b).max(a + motion).max(b + motion) + Vec3::splat(1e-4);
        let entries: Vec<_> = self
            .triangle_meshes
            .iter()
            .filter(|(_, o)| o.cuttable)
            .map(|(&id, o)| {
                let (min, max) = o.mesh.bounds();
                let model = self.objects[&id].transform.model();
                let center = model.transform_point3((min + max) * 0.5);
                let half = (max - min) * 0.5;
                let half_extent = model.x_axis.truncate().abs() * half.x
                    + model.y_axis.truncate().abs() * half.y
                    + model.z_axis.truncate().abs() * half.z;
                (
                    id,
                    crate::spatial::Bounds {
                        center,
                        half_extent,
                    },
                )
            })
            .collect();
        self.cutting_index.update(&entries, false);
        let mut report = CutReport::default();
        let mut candidates = vec![];
        let mut object_tests = 0;
        self.cutting_index.query::<true>(
            &|bounds| {
                (bounds.center + bounds.half_extent).cmpge(min).all()
                    && (bounds.center - bounds.half_extent).cmple(max).all()
            },
            &|_| true,
            &mut candidates,
            &mut report.bvh_nodes,
            &mut object_tests,
        );
        report.object_candidates = candidates.len();
        let mut pending = vec![];
        for id in candidates {
            let source = &self.triangle_meshes[&id];
            let object = &self.objects[&id];
            let inverse = object.transform.model().inverse();
            if !inverse.is_finite() {
                return Err("Noninvertible cutting transform".into());
            }
            if let Some(slice) = source.mesh.cpu()?.slice_prepared(
                inverse.transform_point3(a),
                inverse.transform_point3(b),
                inverse.transform_vector3(motion),
                source.cap_color,
                geometry.and_then(|g| g.get(&id)),
            )? {
                report.bvh_nodes += slice.stats.nodes;
                report.triangle_tests += slice.stats.triangle_tests;
                for piece in slice.pieces {
                    // A box center may be outside its component. A real vertex
                    // furthest from the plane identifies the correct halfspace.
                    let model = object.transform.model();
                    let distance = piece
                        .positions
                        .iter()
                        .map(|&p| normal.dot(model.transform_point3(p) - a))
                        .max_by(|a, b| a.abs().total_cmp(&b.abs()))
                        .ok_or("Empty cut piece")?;
                    let world_sign = distance.signum();
                    let mut transform = object.transform;
                    transform.position += normal * world_sign * separation * 0.5;
                    pending.push((
                        id,
                        piece,
                        transform,
                        object.render_options,
                        source.cap_color,
                        normal * world_sign,
                    ));
                }
                report.removed.push(id);
            }
        }
        if self
            .next_id
            .checked_add(pending.len() as u32)
            .is_none_or(|id| id == INVALID_ID)
        {
            return Err("Object IDs exhausted".into());
        }
        let retained: usize = self
            .triangle_meshes
            .iter()
            .filter(|(id, _)| !report.removed.contains(id))
            .map(|(_, o)| o.mesh.triangle_count())
            .sum();
        if retained + pending.iter().map(|p| p.1.triangles.len()).sum::<usize>() > 200_000 {
            return Err("Scene cutting triangle budget exceeded".into());
        }
        for &id in &report.removed {
            self.remove_object(id);
        }
        for (_, piece, transform, options, cap_color, direction) in pending {
            let id = self.add_triangle_mesh(piece)?;
            self.set_object_transform(id, transform.position, transform.rotation, transform.scale);
            self.set_render_options(id, options);
            self.set_cuttable(id, true, cap_color)?;
            report.pieces.push(id);
            report.piece_directions.push(direction);
        }
        self.cutting_swings.insert(
            cutter,
            (transform.position, transform.position + motion, 0.0),
        );
        Ok(report)
    }
    /// Presentation animation for already cut solids, not a rigid-body solver.
    /// Directions come from the actual split halfspaces, not bounding-box centers.
    fn scatter_cut_pieces(
        &mut self,
        ids: &[u32],
        directions: &[Vec3],
        floor_y: f32,
        distance: f32,
        duration: f32,
    ) {
        for (&id, &normal) in ids.iter().zip(directions) {
            let object = &self.objects[&id];
            let (min, max) = self.triangle_meshes[&id].mesh.bounds();
            let pivot = (min + max) * 0.5;
            let direction = Vec3::new(normal.x, 0.0, normal.z).normalize();
            let sign = if direction.x + direction.z >= 0.0 {
                1.0
            } else {
                -1.0
            };
            self.cutting_scatter.insert(
                id,
                CutScatter {
                    start: object.transform,
                    pivot,
                    direction,
                    angular: Vec3::new(direction.z * 1.15, sign * 0.12, -direction.x * 1.15),
                    floor_y,
                    distance,
                    duration,
                    elapsed: -0.075,
                },
            );
        }
    }
    pub fn cut_with_scatter(
        &mut self,
        cutter: u32,
        motion: Vec3,
        separation: f32,
        floor_y: f32,
        distance: f32,
        duration: f32,
    ) -> Result<CutReport, String> {
        self.cut_with_scatter_geometry(
            cutter,
            motion,
            separation,
            [floor_y, distance, duration],
            None,
        )
    }
    pub fn cut_with_scatter_geometry(
        &mut self,
        cutter: u32,
        motion: Vec3,
        separation: f32,
        scatter: [f32; 3],
        geometry: Option<&BTreeMap<u32, crate::cutting::CutGeometry>>,
    ) -> Result<CutReport, String> {
        let [floor_y, distance, duration] = scatter;
        if !floor_y.is_finite()
            || floor_y.abs() > 10_000.0
            || !distance.is_finite()
            || !(0.0..=3.0).contains(&distance)
            || !duration.is_finite()
            || !(0.05..=3.0).contains(&duration)
        {
            return Err("Invalid fragment animation options".into());
        }
        let blade = self.cutters.get(&cutter).ok_or("Object is not a cutter")?;
        let model = self
            .objects
            .get(&cutter)
            .ok_or("Cutter no longer exists")?
            .transform
            .model();
        let normal = model.transform_vector3(blade[1] - blade[0]).cross(motion);
        let axis = Vec3::new(normal.x, 0.0, normal.z).normalize_or_zero();
        if !axis.is_finite() || axis.length_squared() < 0.5 {
            return Err("Scatter requires a cut with a horizontal normal".into());
        }
        let report = self.cut_with_geometry(cutter, motion, separation, geometry)?;
        self.scatter_cut_pieces(
            &report.pieces,
            &report.piece_directions,
            floor_y,
            distance,
            duration,
        );
        Ok(report)
    }
    pub fn add_cube(&mut self, position: Vec3) -> u32 {
        self.add_object(
            MeshKind::Cube,
            Transform::new(position, Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ONE),
        )
    }
    pub fn add_plane(&mut self, position: Vec3) -> u32 {
        self.add_object(
            MeshKind::Plane,
            Transform::new(position, Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ONE),
        )
    }
    pub fn add_sphere(&mut self, position: Vec3) -> u32 {
        self.add_object(
            MeshKind::Sphere,
            Transform::new(position, Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ONE),
        )
    }
    pub fn clear_scene(&mut self) {
        self.projectiles.clear();
        self.projectile_scratch.clear();
        self.projectile_order.clear();
        self.projectile_pairs.clear();
        self.projectile_contacts = 0;
        self.triangle_meshes.clear();
        self.cutters.clear();
        self.cutting_swings.clear();
        self.cutting_scatter.clear();
        self.cutting_guide = None;
        self.cutting_index = crate::spatial::Bvh::default();
        self.colliders.clear();
        self.collision_dirty = true;
        self.objects.clear();
        self.models.clear();
        self.mirror_object = None;
        self.spins.clear();
        self.static_revision = self.static_revision.wrapping_add(1);
        self.render_revision = self.render_revision.wrapping_add(1);
        self.rotations.clear();
        self.removals
            .retain(|r| matches!(r.kind, RemovalKind::Light));
    }
    pub fn clear_lights(&mut self) {
        self.lights.clear();
        self.light_orbits.clear();
        self.removals
            .retain(|r| matches!(r.kind, RemovalKind::Object));
    }
    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.clear_scene();
        self.clear_lights();
        self.projectiles.clear();
        self.paused = false;
    }
    pub fn remove_object(&mut self, id: u32) -> bool {
        let was_static = self
            .objects
            .get(&id)
            .is_some_and(|o| o.render_options.is_static);
        if self.objects.remove(&id).is_none() {
            return false;
        }
        self.collision_dirty |= self.colliders.remove(&id).is_some();
        self.models.remove(&id);
        self.triangle_meshes.remove(&id);
        self.cutters.remove(&id);
        self.cutting_swings.remove(&id);
        self.cutting_scatter.remove(&id);
        if self
            .cutting_guide
            .is_some_and(|(cutter, guide, _)| cutter == id || guide == id)
        {
            self.cutting_guide = None;
        }
        self.render_revision = self.render_revision.wrapping_add(1);
        if was_static {
            self.static_revision = self.static_revision.wrapping_add(1);
        }
        if self.mirror_object == Some(id) {
            self.mirror_object = None;
        }
        self.spins.remove(&id);
        self.projectiles.remove(&id);
        self.rotations.retain(|r| r.id != id);
        self.removals
            .retain(|r| r.id != id || !matches!(r.kind, RemovalKind::Object));
        true
    }
    pub fn set_object_transform(
        &mut self,
        id: u32,
        position: Vec3,
        rotation: Vec3,
        scale: Vec3,
    ) -> bool {
        let transform = Transform::new(position, rotation, scale);
        if !transform.is_valid() {
            return false;
        }
        let Some(object) = self.objects.get_mut(&id) else {
            return false;
        };
        object.transform = transform;
        object.refresh_render_data();
        self.cutting_swings.remove(&id);
        self.cutting_scatter.remove(&id);
        self.changed(id);
        true
    }
    pub fn set_object_position(&mut self, id: u32, position: Vec3) -> bool {
        if !position.is_finite() {
            return false;
        }
        let Some(object) = self.objects.get_mut(&id) else {
            return false;
        };
        object.transform.position = position;
        object.refresh_render_data();
        self.cutting_swings.remove(&id);
        self.cutting_scatter.remove(&id);
        self.changed(id);
        true
    }
    pub fn set_object_rotation(&mut self, id: u32, rotation: Vec3) -> bool {
        if !rotation.is_finite() {
            return false;
        }
        let Some(object) = self.objects.get_mut(&id) else {
            return false;
        };
        object.transform.rotation = rotation;
        object.refresh_render_data();
        self.cutting_scatter.remove(&id);
        self.changed(id);
        true
    }
    pub fn set_object_scale(&mut self, id: u32, scale: Vec3) -> bool {
        if !scale.is_finite() || scale.abs().min_element() <= 0.0 {
            return false;
        }
        let Some(object) = self.objects.get_mut(&id) else {
            return false;
        };
        object.transform.scale = scale;
        object.refresh_render_data();
        self.cutting_scatter.remove(&id);
        self.changed(id);
        true
    }
    pub fn add_light(&mut self, position: Vec3, color: Vec3) -> Option<u32> {
        if self.lights.len() >= MAX_LIGHTS || !position.is_finite() || !color.is_finite() {
            return None;
        }
        let id = self.allocate_id()?;
        self.lights.insert(id, Light { position, color });
        Some(id)
    }
    pub fn remove_light(&mut self, id: u32) -> bool {
        if self.lights.remove(&id).is_none() {
            return false;
        }
        self.light_orbits.remove(&id);
        self.removals
            .retain(|r| r.id != id || !matches!(r.kind, RemovalKind::Light));
        true
    }
    pub fn set_light(&mut self, id: u32, position: Vec3, color: Vec3) -> bool {
        if !position.is_finite() || !color.is_finite() {
            return false;
        }
        let Some(light) = self.lights.get_mut(&id) else {
            return false;
        };
        *light = Light { position, color };
        true
    }
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)] // Explicit GPU/FFI parameter layout.
    pub fn set_light_orbit(
        &mut self,
        id: u32,
        radius: f32,
        height: f32,
        speed: f32,
        phase: f32,
        pulse: f32,
        color: Vec3,
    ) -> bool {
        if !self.light_exists(id)
            || ![radius, height, speed, phase, pulse]
                .iter()
                .all(|v| v.is_finite())
            || !color.is_finite()
        {
            return false;
        }
        self.light_orbits.insert(
            id,
            LightOrbit {
                radius: radius.max(0.01),
                height,
                speed,
                phase,
                pulse: pulse.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
                color,
            },
        );
        true
    }
    pub fn clear_light_orbit(&mut self, id: u32) -> bool {
        if !self.light_exists(id) {
            return false;
        }
        self.light_orbits.remove(&id);
        true
    }
    pub fn schedule_rotate(
        &mut self,
        id: u32,
        rx_deg: f32,
        ry_deg: f32,
        rz_deg: f32,
        delay: f32,
        duration: f32,
    ) -> bool {
        let degrees = Vec3::new(rx_deg, ry_deg, rz_deg);
        if !self.object_exists(id)
            || !degrees.is_finite()
            || !delay.is_finite()
            || !duration.is_finite()
            || duration <= 0.0
        {
            return false;
        }
        self.rotations.push(RotationAction {
            id,
            start: self.time + delay.max(0.0),
            duration,
            delta: degrees * std::f32::consts::PI / 180.0,
            elapsed: 0.0,
        });
        true
    }
    pub fn schedule_remove_object(&mut self, id: u32, delay: f32) -> bool {
        if !self.object_exists(id) || !delay.is_finite() {
            return false;
        }
        self.removals.push(ScheduledRemoval {
            id,
            at: self.time + delay.max(0.0),
            kind: RemovalKind::Object,
        });
        true
    }
    pub fn schedule_remove_light(&mut self, id: u32, delay: f32) -> bool {
        if !self.light_exists(id) || !delay.is_finite() {
            return false;
        }
        self.removals.push(ScheduledRemoval {
            id,
            at: self.time + delay.max(0.0),
            kind: RemovalKind::Light,
        });
        true
    }
    pub fn update(&mut self, dt: f32) {
        if self.paused || !dt.is_finite() || dt <= 0.0 {
            return;
        }
        let active: Vec<_> = self
            .models
            .iter()
            .filter(|(_, model)| {
                model
                    .playback
                    .as_ref()
                    .is_some_and(|p| p.playing && p.speed != 0.)
            })
            .map(|(&id, _)| id)
            .collect();
        for id in active {
            let mut candidate = self.models[&id].clone();
            let result = match candidate.update(dt) {
                Ok(true) => self.commit_model(id, candidate),
                Ok(false) => Ok(()),
                Err(error) => Err(error),
            };
            if let Err(error) = result {
                if let Some(model) = self.models.get_mut(&id) {
                    model.last_error = Some(error);
                    if let Some(playback) = &mut model.playback {
                        playback.playing = false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        self.time += dt;
        let now = self.time;
        let pending = std::mem::take(&mut self.removals);
        for removal in pending {
            if now < removal.at {
                self.removals.push(removal);
                continue;
            }
            match removal.kind {
                RemovalKind::Object => {
                    self.remove_object(removal.id);
                }
                RemovalKind::Light => {
                    self.remove_light(removal.id);
                }
            }
        }
        self.cutting_swings.retain(|id, (start, end, elapsed)| {
            let Some(object) = self.objects.get_mut(id) else {
                return false;
            };
            *elapsed += dt;
            let t = (*elapsed / 0.24).min(1.0);
            object.transform.position = start.lerp(*end, t * t * (3.0 - 2.0 * t));
            object.refresh_render_data();
            self.collision_dirty |= self.colliders.contains_key(id);
            self.render_revision = self.render_revision.wrapping_add(1);
            if object.render_options.is_static {
                self.static_revision = self.static_revision.wrapping_add(1);
            }
            t < 1.0
        });
        self.cutting_scatter.retain(|id, action| {
            let Some(object) = self.objects.get_mut(id) else {
                return false;
            };
            let Some(geometry) = self.triangle_meshes.get(id) else {
                return false;
            };
            action.elapsed += dt;
            if action.elapsed < 0.0 {
                return true;
            }
            let t = (action.elapsed / action.duration).min(1.0);
            let eased = 1.0 - (1.0 - t).powi(3);
            let mut transform = action.start;
            transform.rotation += action.angular * eased;
            transform.position = Vec3::ZERO;
            let pivot = action.start.model().transform_point3(action.pivot);
            transform.position = pivot + action.direction * action.distance * eased
                - transform.model().transform_point3(action.pivot);
            // Support every vertex on the board after rotation; a small hop settles
            // to exactly floor_y. Pivot rotation does not orbit around the old root.
            let bottom = geometry
                .mesh
                .bottom(transform)
                .expect("Validated cutting support");
            transform.position.y +=
                action.floor_y - bottom + (std::f32::consts::PI * t).sin() * 0.18 * (1.0 - t);
            object.transform = transform;
            object.refresh_render_data();
            self.render_revision = self.render_revision.wrapping_add(1);
            if object.render_options.is_static {
                self.static_revision = self.static_revision.wrapping_add(1);
            }
            self.collision_dirty |= self.colliders.contains_key(id);
            t < 1.0
        });
        self.rotations.retain_mut(|rotation| {
            let Some(object) = self.objects.get_mut(&rotation.id) else {
                return false;
            };
            if now < rotation.start {
                return true;
            }
            let elapsed = (now - rotation.start).min(rotation.duration);
            object.transform.rotation +=
                rotation.delta * ((elapsed - rotation.elapsed) / rotation.duration);
            rotation.elapsed = elapsed;
            object.refresh_render_data();
            self.render_revision = self.render_revision.wrapping_add(1);
            if object.render_options.is_static {
                self.static_revision = self.static_revision.wrapping_add(1);
            }
            elapsed < rotation.duration
        });
        for (id, velocity) in &self.spins {
            if let Some(object) = self.objects.get_mut(id) {
                object.transform.rotation += *velocity * dt;
                // Active projectiles refresh once after both rotation and motion.
                if self.projectiles.contains_key(id) {
                    continue;
                }
                object.refresh_render_data();
                self.render_revision = self.render_revision.wrapping_add(1);
                if object.render_options.is_static {
                    self.static_revision = self.static_revision.wrapping_add(1);
                }
            }
        }
        self.update_projectiles(dt);
        for (id, orbit) in &self.light_orbits {
            if let Some(light) = self.lights.get_mut(id) {
                let angle = now * orbit.speed + orbit.phase;
                light.position = Vec3::new(
                    angle.cos() * orbit.radius,
                    orbit.height + (angle * 1.3).sin() * 0.3,
                    angle.sin() * orbit.radius,
                );
                let pulse =
                    1.0 - orbit.pulse * 0.5 + (now * 2.0 + orbit.phase).sin() * orbit.pulse * 0.5;
                light.color = orbit.color * pulse;
            }
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    #[test]
    fn clearing_scene_and_parking_release_projectile_work() {
        use super::*;
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        let id = scene.add_sphere(Vec3::ZERO);
        assert!(scene.set_projectile(id, Vec3::Y, 12.0, 0.0));
        assert!(scene.set_projectile(id, Vec3::ZERO, 0.0, 0.0));
        assert!(scene.projectiles.is_empty());
        assert!(scene.object_exists(id));
        scene.set_projectile(id, Vec3::Y, 12.0, 0.0);
        scene.update(1.0 / 60.0);
        scene.clear_scene();
        assert!(scene.projectiles.is_empty());
        assert!(scene.projectile_scratch.is_empty());
    }
    #[test]
    #[ignore = "manual release performance comparison"]
    fn benchmark_projectile_solver() {
        use super::*;
        for count in [8, 48, 256] {
            let mut samples = Vec::new();
            for _ in 0..5 {
                let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
                for i in 0..count {
                    let id = scene.add_sphere(Vec3::new(
                        (i % 16) as f32 * 1.3,
                        6.0 + (i / 16) as f32 * 1.1,
                        0.0,
                    ));
                    scene.set_object_scale(id, Vec3::splat(0.38));
                    scene.set_projectile(
                        id,
                        Vec3::new(if i % 2 == 0 { 0.3 } else { -0.3 }, 3.0, 0.0),
                        12.0,
                        0.5,
                    );
                }
                scene.update_projectiles(1.0 / 60.0);
                let start = std::time::Instant::now();
                for _ in 0..240 {
                    scene.update_projectiles(1.0 / 60.0);
                }
                samples.push(start.elapsed().as_secs_f64() * 1e6 / 240.0);
                std::hint::black_box(scene.projectile_contacts);
            }
            samples.sort_by(f64::total_cmp);
            eprintln!("projectiles={count} median_us_per_frame={:.3}", samples[2]);
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn projectile_collision_stops_fast_crossing_and_separates_overlap() {
        use super::*;
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        let a = scene.add_sphere(Vec3::new(-1.0, 4.0, 0.0));
        let b = scene.add_sphere(Vec3::new(1.0, 4.0, 0.0));
        scene.set_object_scale(a, Vec3::splat(0.38));
        scene.set_object_scale(b, Vec3::splat(0.38));
        scene.set_projectile(a, Vec3::X * 25.0, 0.0, 0.0);
        scene.set_projectile(b, -Vec3::X * 25.0, 0.0, 0.0);
        scene.update(0.1);
        assert!(scene.projectiles[&a].0.x < 0.0);
        assert!(scene.projectiles[&b].0.x > 0.0);
        assert!(scene.projectile_contacts > 0);
        assert!(scene.objects[&a].transform.position.x < scene.objects[&b].transform.position.x);
        assert!(
            (scene.objects[&a].transform.position - scene.objects[&b].transform.position).length()
                >= 0.874 - 1e-4
        );
        scene.set_object_position(a, Vec3::new(0.0, 4.0, 0.0));
        scene.set_object_position(b, Vec3::new(0.0, 4.0, 0.0));
        scene.set_projectile(a, Vec3::ZERO, 12.0, 0.0);
        scene.set_projectile(b, Vec3::ZERO, 12.0, 0.0);
        scene.update(1.0 / 60.0);
        assert!(
            (scene.objects[&a].transform.position - scene.objects[&b].transform.position).length()
                >= 0.874 - 1e-4
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn static_revision_invalidates_only_when_static_geometry_changes() {
        use super::*;
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        scene.load_effects_demo();
        let revision = scene.static_revision();
        let geometry = scene.render_revision();
        scene.update(0.5);
        assert_eq!(scene.static_revision(), revision);
        assert_ne!(scene.render_revision(), geometry);
        let id = *scene
            .objects()
            .iter()
            .find(|(_, o)| o.render_options.is_static && o.render_options.casts_shadow)
            .unwrap()
            .0;
        scene.set_object_position(id, Vec3::new(-2.0, 0.0, 0.0));
        assert_ne!(scene.static_revision(), revision);
        let revision = scene.static_revision();
        scene.remove_object(id);
        assert_ne!(scene.static_revision(), revision);
        let geometry = scene.render_revision();
        scene.paused = true;
        scene.update(1.0);
        assert_eq!(scene.render_revision(), geometry);
    }
    #[test]
    fn mirror_follows_its_transform_and_removal() {
        use super::*;
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        scene.load_effects_demo();
        let id = scene.mirror_object.unwrap();
        scene.set_object_position(id, Vec3::new(0.0, 1.0, -5.0));
        let plane = scene.mirror_plane().unwrap();
        assert!((plane.normal.dot(Vec3::new(0.0, 1.0, -5.0)) + plane.distance).abs() < 1e-5);
        assert!(plane.normal.dot(Vec3::Z).abs() > 0.99);
        assert!(!scene.configure_mirror(super::INVALID_ID));
        scene.remove_object(id);
        assert!(scene.mirror_plane().is_none());
    }
    #[test]
    fn normal_transform_preserves_perpendicularity_under_nonuniform_scale() {
        let transform = super::Transform::new(
            glam::Vec3::new(3.0, -2.0, 5.0),
            glam::Vec3::new(0.4, 0.7, -0.2),
            glam::Vec3::new(2.0, 0.5, 3.0),
        );
        let model = transform.model();
        let normal = glam::Vec3::new(1.0, 1.0, 1.0).normalize();
        let tangent = glam::Vec3::new(1.0, -1.0, 0.0);
        let world_normal = super::normal_matrix(model)
            .transform_vector3(normal)
            .normalize();
        let world_tangent = model.transform_vector3(tangent).normalize();
        assert!(world_normal.dot(world_tangent).abs() < 1e-5);
        // The old model-matrix transformation fails this same geometry.
        assert!(
            model
                .transform_vector3(normal)
                .normalize()
                .dot(world_tangent)
                .abs()
                > 0.1
        );
    }
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn animation_is_computed_by_rust_and_finishes_exactly() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        let id = scene.add_cube(Vec3::ZERO);
        assert!(scene.schedule_rotate(id, 0.0, 180.0, 0.0, 1.0, 2.0));
        scene.update(1.0);
        assert_eq!(scene.objects()[&id].transform.rotation, Vec3::ZERO);
        scene.update(1.0);
        assert!(
            (scene.objects()[&id].transform.rotation.y - std::f32::consts::FRAC_PI_2).abs() < 1e-5
        );
        scene.update(3.0);
        assert!((scene.objects()[&id].transform.rotation.y - std::f32::consts::PI).abs() < 1e-5);
        scene.update(3.0);
        assert!((scene.objects()[&id].transform.rotation.y - std::f32::consts::PI).abs() < 1e-5);
    }
    #[test]
    fn removed_and_reset_handles_never_target_new_objects() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        let old = scene.add_cube(Vec3::ZERO);
        scene.schedule_remove_object(old, 2.0);
        scene.remove_object(old);
        let new = scene.add_cube(Vec3::ONE);
        assert_ne!(old, new);
        assert!(!scene.set_object_position(old, Vec3::ZERO));
        scene.update(3.0);
        assert!(scene.object_exists(new));
        scene.clear();
        assert_ne!(new, scene.add_cube(Vec3::ZERO));
    }
    #[test]
    fn lights_enforce_capacity_and_removal_does_not_resurrect_them() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        let first = scene.add_light(Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ONE).unwrap();
        for _ in 1..MAX_LIGHTS {
            assert!(scene.add_light(Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ONE).is_some());
        }
        assert!(scene.add_light(Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ONE).is_none());
        scene.remove_light(first);
        assert!(!scene.set_light_orbit(first, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0, Vec3::ONE));
        assert!(scene.add_light(Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ONE).is_some());
    }
    #[test]
    fn sample_is_stable_and_invalid_values_do_not_corrupt_simulation() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        scene.load_example();
        assert_eq!(scene.objects().len(), 3);
        assert_eq!(scene.lights().len(), 2);
        scene.update(100.0);
        assert_eq!(scene.objects().len(), 3);
        assert_eq!(scene.add_cube(Vec3::splat(f32::NAN)), INVALID_ID);
        let id = *scene.objects().keys().next().unwrap();
        assert!(!scene.set_object_scale(id, Vec3::ZERO));
        scene.update(f32::NAN);
        assert!(scene.objects()[&id].transform.model().is_finite());
    }
    #[test]
    fn scheduled_removals_and_light_orbits_run_without_javascript() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        let cube = scene.add_cube(Vec3::ZERO);
        let light = scene.add_light(Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ONE).unwrap();
        scene.set_light_orbit(light, 2.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0, Vec3::ONE);
        scene.schedule_remove_object(cube, 1.0);
        scene.schedule_remove_light(light, 2.0);
        scene.update(1.0);
        assert!(!scene.object_exists(cube));
        assert!((scene.lights()[&light].position.x - 2.0_f32 * 1.0_f32.cos()).abs() < 1e-5);
        scene.update(1.0);
        assert!(!scene.light_exists(light));
    }

    #[test]
    fn collider_bvh_tracks_transform_removal_clear_and_only_solid_changes() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        let cube = scene.add_cube(Vec3::ZERO);
        let query = crate::collision::Aabb::new(-Vec3::ONE, Vec3::ONE).unwrap();
        assert!(scene.collision_world().query(query, None).is_empty());
        assert!(scene.set_box_collider(cube, Vec3::splat(0.5), Vec3::ZERO));
        assert_eq!(scene.collision_world().query(query, None).len(), 1);
        assert!(!scene.collision_dirty);
        let player = scene.add_cube(Vec3::ZERO);
        scene.set_object_position(player, Vec3::ONE);
        assert!(
            !scene.collision_dirty,
            "Non-solid movement must preserve the cached BVH"
        );
        scene.set_object_transform(cube, Vec3::new(4.0, 0.0, 0.0), Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::splat(2.0));
        assert!(scene.collision_dirty);
        assert!(scene.collision_world().query(query, None).is_empty());
        let far = crate::collision::Aabb::new(Vec3::new(3.5, -0.5, -0.5), Vec3::new(4.5, 0.5, 0.5))
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(scene.collision_world().query(far, None).len(), 1);
        assert!(scene.remove_collider(cube));
        assert!(scene.collision_world().query(far, None).is_empty());
        scene.set_box_collider(cube, Vec3::splat(0.5), Vec3::ZERO);
        scene.remove_object(cube);
        assert!(scene.collision_world().query(far, None).is_empty());
        scene.set_box_collider(player, Vec3::ONE, Vec3::ZERO);
        scene.clear();
        assert!(scene.collision_world().query(query, None).is_empty());
        assert!(!scene.set_box_collider(player, Vec3::ONE, Vec3::ZERO));
        let invalid = scene.add_cube(Vec3::ZERO);
        assert!(!scene.set_box_collider(invalid, Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ZERO));
    }
}


# File: src/shader.wgsl
struct Light {
    position: vec3<f32>,
    _pad_p: f32,
    color: vec3<f32>,
    _pad_c: f32,
};

const LIGHT_COUNT: u32 = 4u;

struct SceneUniforms {
    view_projection: mat4x4<f32>,
    camera_pos: vec3<f32>,
    _pad0: f32,
    lights: array<Light, LIGHT_COUNT>,
    guide_plane: vec4<f32>,
    guide_origin: vec4<f32>,
    guide_axis: vec4<f32>,
};

@group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> scene: SceneUniforms;

struct VertexInput {
    @location(0) position: vec3<f32>,
    @location(1) color: vec3<f32>,
    @location(2) normal: vec3<f32>,
};

struct VertexOutput {
    @builtin(position) pos: vec4<f32>,
    @location(0) color: vec3<f32>,
    @location(1) world_pos: vec3<f32>,
    @location(2) world_normal: vec3<f32>,
    @location(3) @interpolate(flat) material: vec4<f32>,
};

struct InstanceInput {
    @location(3) model0: vec4<f32>,
    @location(4) model1: vec4<f32>,
    @location(5) model2: vec4<f32>,
    @location(6) model3: vec4<f32>,
    @location(7) normal0: vec4<f32>,
    @location(8) normal1: vec4<f32>,
    @location(9) normal2: vec4<f32>,
    @location(10) normal3: vec4<f32>,
    @location(11) material: vec4<f32>,
};
@vertex
fn vs_main(input: VertexInput, instance: InstanceInput) -> VertexOutput {
    var out: VertexOutput;
    let model = mat4x4<f32>(instance.model0, instance.model1, instance.model2, instance.model3);
    let normal_matrix = mat4x4<f32>(instance.normal0, instance.normal1, instance.normal2, instance.normal3);
    out.pos = scene.view_projection * model * vec4<f32>(input.position, 1.0);
    out.color = input.color;
    out.material = instance.material;
    out.world_pos = (model * vec4<f32>(input.position, 1.0)).xyz;
    // Rust supplies the inverse transpose. Normalize after interpolation so
    // non-uniformly scaled smooth surfaces retain the correct normal field.
    out.world_normal = (normal_matrix * vec4<f32>(input.normal, 0.0)).xyz;
    return out;
}

@vertex
fn vs_lines(input: VertexInput) -> VertexOutput {
    var out: VertexOutput;
    out.pos = scene.view_projection * vec4<f32>(input.position, 1.0);
    out.color = input.color;
    out.material = vec4(0.0);
    out.world_pos = input.position;
    out.world_normal = input.normal;
    return out;
}

struct EffectsUniforms {
    shadow_matrices:array<mat4x4<f32>,4>, reflection_matrix:mat4x4<f32>,
    clip_plane:vec4<f32>, params:vec4<f32>,
};
@group(1) @binding(0) var<uniform> effects:EffectsUniforms;
@group(1) @binding(1) var static_shadow:texture_depth_2d;
@group(1) @binding(2) var dynamic_shadow:texture_depth_2d;
@group(1) @binding(3) var shadow_sampler:sampler_comparison;
@group(1) @binding(4) var reflection_texture:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(1) @binding(5) var reflection_sampler:sampler;
@group(1) @binding(6) var static_shadow_1:texture_depth_2d;
@group(1) @binding(7) var dynamic_shadow_1:texture_depth_2d;
@group(1) @binding(8) var static_shadow_2:texture_depth_2d;
@group(1) @binding(9) var dynamic_shadow_2:texture_depth_2d;
@group(1) @binding(10) var static_shadow_3:texture_depth_2d;
@group(1) @binding(11) var dynamic_shadow_3:texture_depth_2d;
fn compare_shadow(index:u32,uv:vec2<f32>,depth:f32)->f32 {
    switch index {
        case 0u: { return min(textureSampleCompareLevel(static_shadow,shadow_sampler,uv,depth),textureSampleCompareLevel(dynamic_shadow,shadow_sampler,uv,depth)); }
        case 1u: { return min(textureSampleCompareLevel(static_shadow_1,shadow_sampler,uv,depth),textureSampleCompareLevel(dynamic_shadow_1,shadow_sampler,uv,depth)); }
        case 2u: { return min(textureSampleCompareLevel(static_shadow_2,shadow_sampler,uv,depth),textureSampleCompareLevel(dynamic_shadow_2,shadow_sampler,uv,depth)); }
        case 3u: { return min(textureSampleCompareLevel(static_shadow_3,shadow_sampler,uv,depth),textureSampleCompareLevel(dynamic_shadow_3,shadow_sampler,uv,depth)); }
        default: { return 1.0; }
    }
}
fn shadow_visibility(index:u32,world:vec3<f32>,normal:vec3<f32>,light_direction:vec3<f32>)->f32 {
    let clip=effects.shadow_matrices[index]*vec4(world,1.0);
    let ndc=clip.xyz/clip.w;
    if (clip.w<=0.0 || ndc.z<=0.0 || ndc.z>=1.0 || any(abs(ndc.xy)>vec2(1.0))) {return 1.0;}
    let uv=ndc.xy*vec2(0.5,-0.5)+vec2(0.5);
    let bias=effects.params.w*(1.0+2.0*(1.0-max(dot(normal,light_direction),0.0)));
    let depth=ndc.z-bias;
    let texel=1.0/vec2<f32>(textureDimensions(static_shadow));
    var visibility=0.0;
    // Combine static and dynamic visibility per texel, then apply 3x3 PCF.
    for(var y=-1;y<=1;y++) {for(var x=-1;x<=1;x++) {
        let sample_uv=uv+vec2<f32>(f32(x),f32(y))*texel;
        visibility+=compare_shadow(index,sample_uv,depth);
    }}
    return visibility/9.0;
}

fn shade_surface(input: VertexOutput) -> vec4<f32> {
    if (effects.params.z>0.5 && dot(effects.clip_plane,vec4(input.world_pos,1.0)) < -0.001) { discard; }
    let normal = normalize(input.world_normal);
    let view_dir = normalize(scene.camera_pos - input.world_pos);
    var result = input.color * 0.1; // ambient

    for (var i: u32 = 0u; i < LIGHT_COUNT; i = i + 1u) {
        let light = scene.lights[i];
        if (all(light.color == vec3<f32>(0.0))) {
            continue;
        }
        let light_delta = light.position - input.world_pos;
        let l_dir = light_delta / max(length(light_delta), 0.00001);
        let diff = max(dot(normal, l_dir), 0.0);
        // Blinn-Phong: an unlit/back-facing surface cannot reflect this light.
        var spec = 0.0;
        if (diff > 0.0 && dot(normal, view_dir) > 0.0) {
            let half_vector = l_dir + view_dir;
            let halfway = half_vector / max(length(half_vector), 0.00001);
            spec = pow(max(dot(normal, halfway), 0.0), 32.0);
        }
        var visibility=1.0;
        if(i<u32(effects.params.x) && input.material.y>0.5) {
            visibility=shadow_visibility(i,input.world_pos,normal,l_dir);
        }
        result += (diff * input.color + spec) * light.color * visibility;
    }

    if(effects.params.y>0.5 && input.material.z>0.5 && input.material.x>0.0) {
        let reflected=effects.reflection_matrix*vec4(input.world_pos,1.0);
        let uv=reflected.xy/reflected.w*vec2(0.5,-0.5)+vec2(0.5);
        if(reflected.w>0.0 && all(uv>=vec2(0.0)) && all(uv<=vec2(1.0))) {
            result=mix(result,textureSampleLevel(reflection_texture,reflection_sampler,uv,0.0).rgb,input.material.x);
        }
    }
    // Intersect the same finite blade sweep with actual visible cuttable surfaces.
    // Regular depth testing keeps hidden fruit hidden; no duplicate contour mesh.
    let along = dot(input.world_pos - scene.guide_origin.xyz, scene.guide_axis.xyz);
    let distance = abs(dot(vec4(input.world_pos, 1.0), scene.guide_plane));
    if (input.material.w > 0.5 && scene.guide_axis.w > 0.0
        && abs(along) <= scene.guide_axis.w
        && input.world_pos.y >= scene.guide_origin.w
        && input.world_pos.y <= scene.guide_origin.y) {
        let band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.022, 0.042, distance);
        result = mix(result, vec3<f32>(0.08, 1.0, 0.025), band);
    }
    return vec4<f32>(result, input.material.x);
}

@fragment
fn fs_color(input: VertexOutput) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
    return vec4<f32>(input.color, 1.0);
}

// Alpha is material metadata only in the offscreen postprocessing path.
@fragment fn fs_main(input: VertexOutput) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
    return vec4(shade_surface(input).rgb, 1.0);
}
struct PostSurface {
    @location(0) color: vec4<f32>,
    @location(1) depth: vec4<f32>,
};
@fragment fn fs_postprocess(input: VertexOutput) -> PostSurface {
    var output: PostSurface;
    output.color = shade_surface(input);
    var encoded = fract(min(input.pos.z, 0.9999999) * vec3(1.0, 255.0, 65025.0));
    encoded -= encoded.yzz * vec3(1.0 / 255.0, 1.0 / 255.0, 0.0);
    output.depth = vec4(encoded, 1.0);
    return output;
}


# File: src/spatial.rs
//! Geometry-only median BVH. Bounds may be planar or even points.
use glam::Vec3;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub(crate) struct Bounds {
    pub center: Vec3,
    pub half_extent: Vec3,
}
impl Bounds {
    fn union(self, other: Self) -> Self {
        let min = (self.center - self.half_extent).min(other.center - other.half_extent);
        let max = (self.center + self.half_extent).max(other.center + other.half_extent);
        let center = (min + max) * 0.5;
        let half_extent = (max - min) * 0.5;
        // Parent bounds must not shrink due to floating-point reconstruction.
        Self {
            center,
            half_extent: half_extent + (center.abs() + half_extent) * (2.0 * f32::EPSILON),
        }
    }
}

struct Node {
    bounds: Bounds,
    kind: NodeKind,
}
enum NodeKind {
    Leaf(u32),
    Branch(usize, usize),
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct Bvh {
    nodes: Vec<Node>,
    ids: Vec<u32>,
}
impl Bvh {
    /// IDs in input must be unique and ascending. Returns true for a topology rebuild;
    /// unchanged membership only refits bounds, including all internal nodes.
    pub fn update(&mut self, entries: &[(u32, Bounds)], force_rebuild: bool) -> bool {
        let same_ids = self.ids.len() == entries.len()
            && self
                .ids
                .iter()
                .zip(entries)
                .all(|(id, entry)| *id == entry.0);
        if !force_rebuild && same_ids {
            for index in (0..self.nodes.len()).rev() {
                self.nodes[index].bounds = match self.nodes[index].kind {
                    NodeKind::Leaf(id) => {
                        entries[entries.binary_search_by_key(&id, |e| e.0).unwrap()].1
                    }
                    NodeKind::Branch(left, right) => {
                        self.nodes[left].bounds.union(self.nodes[right].bounds)
                    }
                };
            }
            return false;
        }
        self.ids = entries.iter().map(|e| e.0).collect();
        self.nodes.clear();
        if !entries.is_empty() {
            self.build(&mut entries.to_vec());
        }
        true
    }
    fn build(&mut self, entries: &mut [(u32, Bounds)]) -> usize {
        let bounds = entries
            .iter()
            .skip(1)
            .fold(entries[0].1, |b, e| b.union(e.1));
        let index = self.nodes.len();
        self.nodes.push(Node {
            bounds,
            kind: NodeKind::Leaf(entries[0].0),
        });
        if entries.len() > 1 {
            let axis = if bounds.half_extent.x >= bounds.half_extent.y
                && bounds.half_extent.x >= bounds.half_extent.z
            {
                0
            } else if bounds.half_extent.y >= bounds.half_extent.z {
                1
            } else {
                2
            };
            let middle = entries.len() / 2;
            entries.select_nth_unstable_by(middle, |a, b| {
                a.1.center[axis]
                    .total_cmp(&b.1.center[axis])
                    .then(a.0.cmp(&b.0))
            });
            let (left, right) = entries.split_at_mut(middle);
            let left = self.build(left);
            let right = self.build(right);
            self.nodes[index].kind = NodeKind::Branch(left, right);
        }
        index
    }
    /// Internal bounds and eligible object bounds are counted separately.
    pub fn query<const TRACK: bool>(
        &self,
        intersects: &impl Fn(Bounds) -> bool,
        eligible: &impl Fn(u32) -> bool,
        output: &mut Vec<u32>,
        nodes_visited: &mut usize,
        object_tests: &mut usize,
    ) {
        if !self.nodes.is_empty() {
            self.query_node::<TRACK>(0, intersects, eligible, output, nodes_visited, object_tests);
        }
    }
    fn query_node<const TRACK: bool>(
        &self,
        index: usize,
        intersects: &impl Fn(Bounds) -> bool,
        eligible: &impl Fn(u32) -> bool,
        output: &mut Vec<u32>,
        nodes_visited: &mut usize,
        object_tests: &mut usize,
    ) {
        let node = &self.nodes[index];
        if TRACK {
            *nodes_visited += 1;
        }
        match node.kind {
            NodeKind::Leaf(id) => {
                if eligible(id) {
                    if TRACK {
                        *object_tests += 1;
                    }
                    if intersects(node.bounds) {
                        output.push(id);
                    }
                }
            }
            NodeKind::Branch(left, right) => {
                if intersects(node.bounds) {
                    self.query_node::<TRACK>(
                        left,
                        intersects,
                        eligible,
                        output,
                        nodes_visited,
                        object_tests,
                    );
                    self.query_node::<TRACK>(
                        right,
                        intersects,
                        eligible,
                        output,
                        nodes_visited,
                        object_tests,
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn planar_and_point_bounds_survive_refit_and_membership_changes() {
        let mut tree = Bvh::default();
        let bounds = |x| Bounds {
            center: Vec3::new(x, 0.0, 0.0),
            half_extent: Vec3::ZERO,
        };
        let mut entries = [
            (2, bounds(0.0)),
            (
                7,
                Bounds {
                    center: Vec3::new(10.0, 0.0, 0.0),
                    half_extent: Vec3::new(1.0, 0.0, 1.0),
                },
            ),
        ];
        assert!(tree.update(&entries, false));
        let query = |tree: &Bvh| {
            let mut output = Vec::new();
            let (mut nodes, mut objects) = (0, 0);
            tree.query::<true>(
                &|b| b.center.x - b.half_extent.x <= 1.0 && b.center.x + b.half_extent.x >= -1.0,
                &|_| true,
                &mut output,
                &mut nodes,
                &mut objects,
            );
            output.sort_unstable();
            output
        };
        assert_eq!(query(&tree), vec![2]);
        entries[0].1 = bounds(20.0);
        entries[1].1.center.x = 0.0;
        assert!(!tree.update(&entries, false));
        assert_eq!(query(&tree), vec![7]);
        assert!(tree.update(&entries[1..], false));
        assert_eq!(query(&tree), vec![7]);
        assert!(tree.update(&[], false));
        assert!(query(&tree).is_empty());
    }
}


# File: src/test_scenes.rs
//! Native test fixtures only. Browser examples live in examples/<name>/scene.js.
use crate::scene::{MeshKind, RenderOptions, SceneManager, Transform};
use glam::Vec3;
impl SceneManager {
    pub fn load_culling_demo(&mut self) {
        self.clear();
        for x in 0..20 {
            for y in 0..25 {
                for z in 0..20 {
                    let position = Vec3::new(
                        (x as f32 - 9.5) * 3.0,
                        (y as f32 - 12.0) * 1.8,
                        (z as f32 - 9.5) * 3.0,
                    );
                    self.add_object(
                        MeshKind::Cube,
                        Transform::new(position, Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::splat(0.65)),
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        for position in [
            Vec3::new(-20.0, 20.0, -20.0),
            Vec3::new(20.0, 20.0, 20.0),
            Vec3::new(-20.0, -20.0, 20.0),
            Vec3::new(20.0, -20.0, -20.0),
        ] {
            self.add_light(position, Vec3::splat(0.35));
        }
    }
    pub fn load_effects_demo(&mut self) {
        self.clear();
        self.paused = false;
        let static_options = RenderOptions {
            is_static: true,
            ..Default::default()
        };
        let floor = self.add_object(
            MeshKind::Plane,
            Transform::new(
                Vec3::new(0.0, -1.0, 0.0),
                Vec3::ZERO,
                Vec3::new(12.0, 1.0, 12.0),
            ),
        );
        self.set_render_options(
            floor,
            RenderOptions {
                casts_shadow: false,
                ..static_options
            },
        );
        let mirror = self.add_object(
            MeshKind::Plane,
            Transform::new(
                Vec3::new(0.0, 0.5, -3.0),
                Vec3::new(std::f32::consts::FRAC_PI_2, 0.0, 0.0),
                Vec3::new(5.5, 1.0, 3.0),
            ),
        );
        self.mirror_object = Some(mirror);
        self.set_render_options(
            mirror,
            RenderOptions {
                casts_shadow: false,
                receives_shadow: false,
                reflectivity: 0.94,
                ..static_options
            },
        );
        for position in [
            Vec3::new(-2.3, -0.3, 0.0),
            Vec3::new(2.2, -0.5, 1.2),
            Vec3::new(-1.5, -0.5, -1.8),
        ] {
            let id = self.add_cube(position);
            self.set_render_options(id, static_options);
        }
        // Thin frame makes the finite mirror surface easy to identify.
        for (position, scale) in [
            (Vec3::new(0.0, 2.08, -3.0), Vec3::new(5.8, 0.16, 0.18)),
            (Vec3::new(0.0, -1.08, -3.0), Vec3::new(5.8, 0.16, 0.18)),
            (Vec3::new(-2.83, 0.5, -3.0), Vec3::new(0.16, 3.3, 0.18)),
            (Vec3::new(2.83, 0.5, -3.0), Vec3::new(0.16, 3.3, 0.18)),
        ] {
            let id = self.add_object(MeshKind::Cube, Transform::new(position, Vec3::ZERO, scale));
            self.set_render_options(id, static_options);
        }
        let cube = self.add_object(
            MeshKind::Cube,
            Transform::new(
                Vec3::new(0.0, 0.1, 0.0),
                Vec3::ZERO,
                Vec3::new(1.2, 1.5, 1.0),
            ),
        );
        self.set_object_spin(cube, Vec3::new(0.25, 0.8, 0.1));
        let sphere = self.add_object(
            MeshKind::Sphere,
            Transform::new(
                Vec3::new(1.8, 0.5, -1.2),
                Vec3::ZERO,
                Vec3::new(0.5, 1.0, 0.5),
            ),
        );
        self.set_object_spin(sphere, Vec3::new(0.0, 0.0, 0.55));
        // Warm key light and blue fill; both illuminate the scene and its reflection.
        self.add_light(Vec3::new(-2.5, 3.0, -1.5), Vec3::new(0.8, 0.224, 0.056));
        self.add_light(Vec3::new(2.5, 2.0, 2.0), Vec3::new(0.052, 0.234, 0.65));
    }
    pub fn load_example(&mut self) {
        self.clear();
        self.add_object(
            MeshKind::Plane,
            Transform::new(
                Vec3::new(0.0, -0.6, 0.0),
                Vec3::ZERO,
                Vec3::new(8.0, 1.0, 8.0),
            ),
        );
        self.add_cube(Vec3::ZERO);
        self.add_object(
            MeshKind::Sphere,
            Transform::new(Vec3::new(1.8, 0.4, 0.2), Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::splat(0.6)),
        );
        self.add_light(Vec3::new(2.5, 3.0, 2.5), Vec3::ONE);
        self.add_light(Vec3::new(-2.0, 1.5, -1.5), Vec3::new(0.2, 0.6, 1.0));
    }
}


# File: src/visibility.rs
//! CPU visibility and render extraction; shared by native tests and WASM.
use crate::scene::{MeshKind, SceneManager, Transform};
use glam::{Mat4, Vec3, Vec4};

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
pub struct InstanceData {
    pub model: [[f32; 4]; 4],
    pub normal_matrix: [[f32; 4]; 4],
    pub material: [f32; 4],
}

pub(crate) struct ObjectRenderData {
    instance: InstanceData,
    center: Vec3,
    half_extent: Vec3,
}
impl ObjectRenderData {
    pub(crate) fn plane_normal(&self) -> Vec3 {
        Mat4::from_cols_array_2d(&self.instance.normal_matrix)
            .transform_vector3(Vec3::Y)
            .normalize()
    }
    pub(crate) fn intersects(&self, frustum: &Frustum) -> bool {
        frustum.intersects_aabb(self.center, self.half_extent)
    }
    pub(crate) fn new(mesh: MeshKind, transform: Transform) -> Self {
        let local = match mesh {
            MeshKind::Cube => Vec3::splat(0.5),
            MeshKind::Plane => Vec3::new(0.5, 0.0, 0.5),
            MeshKind::Sphere => Vec3::ONE,
            MeshKind::Imported(_) => Vec3::ZERO,
        };
        Self::with_bounds(transform, -local, local)
    }
    pub(crate) fn with_bounds(transform: Transform, min: Vec3, max: Vec3) -> Self {
        let model = transform.model();
        let local = (max - min) * 0.5;
        Self {
            instance: InstanceData {
                model: model.to_cols_array_2d(),
                normal_matrix: crate::scene::normal_matrix(model).to_cols_array_2d(),
                material: [0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0],
            },
            center: model.transform_point3((max + min) * 0.5),
            half_extent: model.x_axis.truncate().abs() * local.x
                + model.y_axis.truncate().abs() * local.y
                + model.z_axis.truncate().abs() * local.z,
        }
    }
}

pub struct Frustum {
    planes: [Vec4; 6],
    tolerance: [f32; 6],
}
impl Frustum {
    pub fn from_view_projection(matrix: Mat4) -> Self {
        let rows = matrix.transpose();
        let (x, y, z, w) = (rows.x_axis, rows.y_axis, rows.z_axis, rows.w_axis);
        // WebGPU clip volume: -w <= x,y <= w; 0 <= z <= w.
        let planes = [w + x, w - x, w + y, w - y, z, w - z];
        Self {
            tolerance: planes.map(|p| 1e-5 * p.truncate().length()),
            planes,
        }
    }
    pub fn intersects_aabb(&self, center: Vec3, half_extent: Vec3) -> bool {
        self.planes
            .iter()
            .zip(self.tolerance)
            .all(|(plane, tolerance)| {
                let normal = plane.truncate();
                let distance = normal.dot(center) + plane.w;
                let radius = normal.abs().dot(half_extent);
                // Conservative tolerance, scaled for unnormalized planes.
                distance + radius >= -tolerance
            })
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum RenderPassKind {
    Main,
    Reflection,
    ShadowStatic,
    ShadowDynamic,
}
impl RenderPassKind {
    pub fn index(self) -> usize {
        self as usize
    }
}

/// Query work is per extraction; rebuild counters include only tracked synchronizations.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Default, Debug)]
pub struct CullingStats {
    pub nodes_visited: usize,
    pub object_tests: usize,
    pub static_rebuilds: usize,
    pub dynamic_rebuilds: usize,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct VisibilityIndex {
    static_tree: crate::spatial::Bvh,
    dynamic_tree: crate::spatial::Bvh,
    static_revision: Option<u64>,
    render_revision: Option<u64>,
    totals: [usize; 4],
    static_rebuilds: usize,
    dynamic_rebuilds: usize,
}
impl VisibilityIndex {
    pub(crate) fn sync(&mut self, scene: &SceneManager, collect_stats: bool) {
        if self.render_revision == Some(scene.render_revision()) {
            return;
        }
        let rebuild_static = self.static_revision != Some(scene.static_revision());
        let mut statics = Vec::new();
        let mut dynamics = Vec::new();
        self.totals = [0; 4];
        for (&id, object) in scene.objects() {
            for pass in [
                RenderPassKind::Main,
                RenderPassKind::Reflection,
                RenderPassKind::ShadowStatic,
                RenderPassKind::ShadowDynamic,
            ] {
                self.totals[pass.index()] += usize::from(included(scene, id, object, pass));
            }
            let data = &object.render_data;
            let entry = (
                id,
                crate::spatial::Bounds {
                    center: data.center,
                    half_extent: data.half_extent,
                },
            );
            if object.render_options.is_static {
                if rebuild_static {
                    statics.push(entry);
                }
            } else {
                dynamics.push(entry);
            }
        }
        if rebuild_static {
            self.static_tree.update(&statics, true);
            if collect_stats {
                self.static_rebuilds += 1;
            }
            self.static_revision = Some(scene.static_revision());
        }
        let rebuilt = self.dynamic_tree.update(&dynamics, false);
        if collect_stats && rebuilt {
            self.dynamic_rebuilds += 1;
        }
        self.render_revision = Some(scene.render_revision());
    }
    fn query<const TRACK: bool>(
        &self,
        scene: &SceneManager,
        frustum: &Frustum,
        pass: RenderPassKind,
        output: &mut Vec<u32>,
    ) -> CullingStats {
        let mut stats = CullingStats {
            static_rebuilds: self.static_rebuilds,
            dynamic_rebuilds: self.dynamic_rebuilds,
            ..Default::default()
        };
        let intersects =
            |b: crate::spatial::Bounds| frustum.intersects_aabb(b.center, b.half_extent);
        let eligible = |id| included(scene, id, &scene.objects()[&id], pass);
        if pass != RenderPassKind::ShadowDynamic {
            self.static_tree.query::<TRACK>(
                &intersects,
                &eligible,
                output,
                &mut stats.nodes_visited,
                &mut stats.object_tests,
            );
        }
        if pass != RenderPassKind::ShadowStatic {
            self.dynamic_tree.query::<TRACK>(
                &intersects,
                &eligible,
                output,
                &mut stats.nodes_visited,
                &mut stats.object_tests,
            );
        }
        output.sort_unstable();
        stats
    }
}

fn included(
    scene: &SceneManager,
    id: u32,
    object: &crate::scene::SceneObject,
    pass: RenderPassKind,
) -> bool {
    let options = object.render_options;
    match pass {
        RenderPassKind::Main => true,
        RenderPassKind::Reflection => Some(id) != scene.mirror_object,
        RenderPassKind::ShadowStatic => options.casts_shadow && options.is_static,
        RenderPassKind::ShadowDynamic => options.casts_shadow && !options.is_static,
    }
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct RenderQueue {
    pub batches: [Vec<InstanceData>; 3],
    pub imported: std::collections::BTreeMap<u32, Vec<InstanceData>>,
    pub total: usize,
    pub visible: usize,
    pub culling_stats: CullingStats,
    pub collect_stats: bool,
}
impl RenderQueue {
    pub fn extract(&mut self, scene: &SceneManager, view_projection: Mat4, culling: bool) {
        self.extract_pass(scene, view_projection, culling, RenderPassKind::Main);
    }
    pub fn extract_pass(
        &mut self,
        scene: &SceneManager,
        view_projection: Mat4,
        culling: bool,
        pass: RenderPassKind,
    ) {
        for batch in &mut self.batches {
            batch.clear();
        }
        self.imported.retain(|id, _| scene.object_exists(*id));
        for batch in self.imported.values_mut() {
            batch.clear();
        }
        self.total = 0;
        self.visible = 0;
        let frustum = Frustum::from_view_projection(view_projection);
        let mut candidates = Vec::new();
        self.culling_stats = CullingStats::default();
        if culling {
            let index = scene.visibility_index(self.collect_stats);
            self.total = if pass == RenderPassKind::Reflection {
                index.totals[RenderPassKind::Main.index()]
                    - usize::from(
                        scene
                            .mirror_object
                            .is_some_and(|id| scene.object_exists(id)),
                    )
            } else {
                index.totals[pass.index()]
            };
            self.culling_stats = if self.collect_stats {
                index.query::<true>(scene, &frustum, pass, &mut candidates)
            } else {
                index.query::<false>(scene, &frustum, pass, &mut candidates)
            };
        } else {
            candidates.extend(
                scene
                    .objects()
                    .iter()
                    .filter_map(|(&id, object)| included(scene, id, object, pass).then_some(id)),
            );
            self.total = candidates.len();
        }
        for id in &candidates {
            let object = &scene.objects()[id];
            let options = object.render_options;
            let data = &object.render_data;
            let index = match object.mesh {
                MeshKind::Cube => 0,
                MeshKind::Plane => 1,
                MeshKind::Sphere => 2,
                MeshKind::Imported(_) => 3,
            };
            let mut instance = data.instance;
            instance.material = [
                options.reflectivity,
                if options.receives_shadow { 1.0 } else { 0.0 },
                if Some(*id) == scene.mirror_object {
                    1.0
                } else {
                    0.0
                },
                if scene.is_cuttable(*id) { 1.0 } else { 0.0 },
            ];
            if let MeshKind::Imported(mesh_id) = object.mesh {
                self.imported.entry(mesh_id).or_default().push(instance);
            } else {
                self.batches[index].push(instance);
            }
            self.visible += 1;
        }
    }
    pub fn draw_calls(&self) -> usize {
        self.batches
            .iter()
            .filter(|batch| !batch.is_empty())
            .count()
            + self
                .imported
                .values()
                .filter(|batch| !batch.is_empty())
                .count()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn assert_brute_force(scene: &SceneManager, matrix: Mat4, pass: RenderPassKind, culling: bool) {
        let frustum = Frustum::from_view_projection(matrix);
        let mut expected = RenderQueue::default();
        for (&id, object) in scene.objects() {
            let options = object.render_options;
            let eligible = match pass {
                RenderPassKind::Main => true,
                RenderPassKind::Reflection => Some(id) != scene.mirror_object,
                RenderPassKind::ShadowStatic => options.is_static && options.casts_shadow,
                RenderPassKind::ShadowDynamic => !options.is_static && options.casts_shadow,
            };
            if !eligible {
                continue;
            }
            expected.total += 1;
            if culling && !object.render_data.intersects(&frustum) {
                continue;
            }
            expected.visible += 1;
            let mut instance = object.render_data.instance;
            let options = object.render_options;
            instance.material = [
                options.reflectivity,
                if options.receives_shadow { 1.0 } else { 0.0 },
                if Some(id) == scene.mirror_object {
                    1.0
                } else {
                    0.0
                },
                0.0,
            ];
            match object.mesh {
                MeshKind::Cube => expected.batches[0].push(instance),
                MeshKind::Plane => expected.batches[1].push(instance),
                MeshKind::Sphere => expected.batches[2].push(instance),
                MeshKind::Imported(mesh) => {
                    expected.imported.entry(mesh).or_default().push(instance)
                }
            }
        }
        for track in [false, true] {
            let mut actual = RenderQueue {
                collect_stats: track,
                ..Default::default()
            };
            actual.extract_pass(scene, matrix, culling, pass);
            assert_eq!(
                (actual.total, actual.visible),
                (expected.total, expected.visible)
            );
            for (a, b) in actual.batches.iter().zip(&expected.batches) {
                assert_instances(a, b);
            }
            assert_eq!(
                actual.imported.values().filter(|v| !v.is_empty()).count(),
                expected.imported.len()
            );
            for (id, batch) in &expected.imported {
                assert_instances(&actual.imported[id], batch);
            }
            if !track || !culling {
                assert_eq!(
                    (
                        actual.culling_stats.nodes_visited,
                        actual.culling_stats.object_tests
                    ),
                    (0, 0)
                );
            }
        }
    }
    fn assert_instances(actual: &[InstanceData], expected: &[InstanceData]) {
        assert_eq!(actual.len(), expected.len());
        for (a, b) in actual.iter().zip(expected) {
            assert_eq!(a.model, b.model);
            assert_eq!(a.normal_matrix, b.normal_matrix);
            assert_eq!(a.material, b.material);
        }
    }
    fn compare_passes(scene: &SceneManager) {
        let reflected_view = Mat4::from_scale(Vec3::new(1.0, -1.0, 1.0))
            * Mat4::from_translation(Vec3::new(-2.0, -1.0, 0.0));
        for matrix in [
            camera(),
            camera() * reflected_view,
            crate::render_math::shadow_view_projection(Vec3::new(-3.0, 7.0, 4.0)),
            camera() * Mat4::from_translation(Vec3::new(-200.0, 0.0, 0.0)),
        ] {
            for pass in [
                RenderPassKind::Main,
                RenderPassKind::Reflection,
                RenderPassKind::ShadowStatic,
                RenderPassKind::ShadowDynamic,
            ] {
                for culling in [false, true] {
                    assert_brute_force(scene, matrix, pass, culling);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn bvh_matches_brute_force_across_passes_mutations_and_planar_imports() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        let floor = scene.add_plane(Vec3::new(0.0, 0.0, 2.0));
        scene.set_render_options(
            floor,
            crate::scene::RenderOptions {
                reflectivity: 0.8,
                is_static: true,
                ..Default::default()
            },
        );
        scene.configure_mirror(floor);
        for i in 0..180 {
            let mesh = [MeshKind::Cube, MeshKind::Plane, MeshKind::Sphere][i % 3];
            let id = scene.add_object(
                mesh,
                Transform::new(
                    Vec3::new(
                        (i % 19) as f32 - 9.0,
                        (i % 7) as f32 - 3.0,
                        (i % 23) as f32 - 5.0,
                    ),
                    Vec3::new(0.3, 0.7, -0.2),
                    Vec3::new(-1.5, 0.2, 2.0),
                ),
            );
            scene.set_render_options(
                id,
                crate::scene::RenderOptions {
                    is_static: i % 2 == 0,
                    casts_shadow: i % 5 != 0,
                    receives_shadow: i % 3 == 0,
                    reflectivity: 0.25,
                },
            );
        }
        let imported = scene.add_imported(Vec3::new(2.0, 0.0, 1.0), Vec3::new(4.0, 0.0, 3.0));
        // Multiple object IDs share one imported batch; spatial traversal order
        // must not reorder its instances or change mirrored winding matrices.
        scene.add_object(
            MeshKind::Imported(imported),
            Transform::new(
                Vec3::new(0.0, 0.0, 2.0),
                Vec3::ZERO,
                Vec3::new(-1.0, 1.0, 1.0),
            ),
        );
        scene.add_object(
            MeshKind::Imported(imported),
            Transform::new(Vec3::new(1.0, 0.0, 3.0), Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ONE),
        );
        scene.set_object_transform(
            imported,
            Vec3::new(-2.0, 0.0, 1.0),
            Vec3::new(0.3, 0.2, 0.8),
            Vec3::new(-2.0, 1.0, 0.5),
        );
        compare_passes(&scene);
        scene.set_object_position(imported, Vec3::new(100.0, 0.0, 0.0));
        compare_passes(&scene);
        scene.set_render_options(
            imported,
            crate::scene::RenderOptions {
                is_static: true,
                casts_shadow: false,
                receives_shadow: false,
                reflectivity: 0.6,
            },
        );
        compare_passes(&scene);
        scene.set_render_options(imported, crate::scene::RenderOptions::default());
        scene.mirror_object = None;
        compare_passes(&scene);
        scene.remove_object(imported);
        scene.remove_object(floor);
        compare_passes(&scene);
        scene.clear_scene();
        compare_passes(&scene);
        scene.add_cube(Vec3::new(0.0, 0.0, 2.0));
        compare_passes(&scene);
    }
    #[test]
    fn sparse_bvh_prunes_objects_and_refits_without_static_rebuilds() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        scene.load_culling_demo();
        let static_id = *scene.objects().keys().next().unwrap();
        scene.set_render_options(
            static_id,
            crate::scene::RenderOptions {
                is_static: true,
                ..Default::default()
            },
        );
        let dynamic_id = *scene.objects().keys().nth(1).unwrap();
        let mut queue = RenderQueue {
            collect_stats: true,
            ..Default::default()
        };
        queue.extract(&scene, camera(), true);
        let stats = queue.culling_stats;
        assert!(stats.object_tests < 1000, "{:?}", stats);
        assert!(stats.nodes_visited > 0);
        for matrix in [
            camera(),
            camera() * Mat4::from_translation(Vec3::new(-100.0, 0.0, 0.0)),
        ] {
            queue.extract(&scene, matrix, true);
            assert_eq!(queue.culling_stats.static_rebuilds, stats.static_rebuilds);
            assert_eq!(queue.culling_stats.dynamic_rebuilds, stats.dynamic_rebuilds);
        }
        scene.set_object_position(dynamic_id, Vec3::new(0.0, 0.0, 2.0));
        queue.extract(&scene, camera(), true);
        assert_eq!(queue.culling_stats.static_rebuilds, stats.static_rebuilds);
        assert_eq!(queue.culling_stats.dynamic_rebuilds, stats.dynamic_rebuilds);
        assert_brute_force(&scene, camera(), RenderPassKind::Main, true);
        scene.set_object_position(static_id, Vec3::new(0.0, 0.0, 2.0));
        queue.extract(&scene, camera(), true);
        assert_eq!(
            queue.culling_stats.static_rebuilds,
            stats.static_rebuilds + 1
        );
        scene.remove_object(dynamic_id);
        queue.extract(&scene, camera(), true);
        assert_eq!(
            queue.culling_stats.dynamic_rebuilds,
            stats.dynamic_rebuilds + 1
        );
        queue.extract(&scene, camera(), false);
        assert_eq!((queue.total, queue.visible), (9999, 9999));
        assert_eq!(
            (
                queue.culling_stats.nodes_visited,
                queue.culling_stats.object_tests
            ),
            (0, 0)
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn disabled_diagnostics_do_not_count_rebuilds_or_queries() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        let id = scene.add_cube(Vec3::ZERO);
        scene.set_render_options(
            id,
            crate::scene::RenderOptions {
                is_static: true,
                ..Default::default()
            },
        );
        let mut queue = RenderQueue::default();
        queue.extract(&scene, camera(), true);
        scene.set_object_position(id, Vec3::X);
        queue.extract(&scene, camera(), true);
        {
            let index = scene.visibility_index(false);
            assert_eq!((index.static_rebuilds, index.dynamic_rebuilds), (0, 0));
        }
        assert_eq!(
            (
                queue.culling_stats.nodes_visited,
                queue.culling_stats.object_tests
            ),
            (0, 0)
        );
        queue.collect_stats = true;
        scene.set_object_position(id, Vec3::ZERO);
        queue.extract(&scene, camera(), true);
        assert_eq!(queue.culling_stats.static_rebuilds, 1);
        queue.collect_stats = false;
        scene.set_object_position(id, Vec3::X);
        queue.extract(&scene, camera(), true);
        assert_eq!(scene.visibility_index(false).static_rebuilds, 1);
    }
    fn camera() -> Mat4 {
        glam::camera::lh::proj::directx::perspective(1.0, 1.0, 0.1, 10.0)
    }
    #[test]
    fn ssr_reflectivity_is_independent_of_planar_mirror_registration() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        let floor = scene.add_plane(Vec3::ZERO);
        let cube = scene.add_cube(Vec3::ZERO);
        for id in [floor, cube] {
            assert!(scene.set_render_options(
                id,
                crate::scene::RenderOptions {
                    reflectivity: 0.85,
                    ..Default::default()
                }
            ));
        }
        let mut queue = RenderQueue::default();
        queue.extract(&scene, Mat4::IDENTITY, false);
        for batch in &queue.batches[..2] {
            assert_eq!(batch[0].material[0], 0.85);
            assert_eq!(batch[0].material[2], 0.0);
        }
        assert!(scene.configure_mirror(floor));
        queue.extract(&scene, Mat4::IDENTITY, false);
        assert_eq!(queue.batches[1][0].material[2], 1.0);
        assert_eq!(queue.batches[0][0].material[2], 0.0);
    }
    #[test]
    fn pass_filtering_partitions_casters_and_excludes_mirror_feedback() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        scene.load_effects_demo();
        let mut queue = RenderQueue::default();
        queue.extract_pass(&scene, Mat4::IDENTITY, false, RenderPassKind::ShadowStatic);
        assert_eq!(queue.visible, 7);
        queue.extract_pass(&scene, Mat4::IDENTITY, false, RenderPassKind::ShadowDynamic);
        assert_eq!(queue.visible, 2);
        queue.extract_pass(&scene, Mat4::IDENTITY, false, RenderPassKind::Reflection);
        assert_eq!(queue.visible, 10);
        queue.extract(&scene, Mat4::IDENTITY, false);
        assert_eq!(queue.visible, 11);
        // A caster outside the main frustum remains eligible in the light frustum.
        let id = scene.add_cube(Vec3::new(5.0, 0.0, 0.0));
        let main = Frustum::from_view_projection(camera());
        assert!(!scene.objects()[&id].render_data.intersects(&main));
        let light = Frustum::from_view_projection(crate::render_math::shadow_view_projection(
            Vec3::new(-3.0, 7.0, 4.0),
        ));
        assert!(scene.objects()[&id].render_data.intersects(&light));
    }
    #[test]
    fn spatial_demo_spans_the_world_and_camera_can_leave_it() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        scene.load_culling_demo();
        assert_eq!(scene.objects().len(), 10000);
        assert_eq!(scene.lights().len(), 4);
        let mut queue = RenderQueue::default();
        queue.extract(&scene, camera(), true);
        assert!(queue.visible > 0 && queue.visible < queue.total);
        let outside = glam::camera::lh::view::look_at_mat4(
            Vec3::new(100.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            Vec3::new(200.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            Vec3::Y,
        );
        queue.extract(&scene, camera() * outside, true);
        assert_eq!(queue.visible, 0);
        queue.extract(&scene, camera() * outside, false);
        assert_eq!((queue.visible, queue.draw_calls()), (10000, 1));
    }
    #[test]
    fn moving_camera_and_intersecting_bounds_do_not_lose_visible_objects() {
        let projection = camera();
        let view = glam::camera::lh::view::look_at_mat4(
            Vec3::new(5.0, 2.0, -3.0),
            Vec3::new(5.0, 2.0, 0.0),
            Vec3::Y,
        );
        let f = Frustum::from_view_projection(projection * view);
        assert!(f.intersects_aabb(Vec3::new(5.0, 2.0, 0.0), Vec3::splat(0.1)));
        assert!(!f.intersects_aabb(Vec3::new(5.0, 2.0, -5.0), Vec3::splat(0.1)));
        // The center is outside, but the large object's bounds intersect the view.
        assert!(Frustum::from_view_projection(projection)
            .intersects_aabb(Vec3::new(2.0, 0.0, 1.0), Vec3::new(2.0, 0.1, 0.1),));
    }
    #[test]
    fn animation_refreshes_cached_instance_and_normal_matrices() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        let id = scene.add_cube(Vec3::new(0.0, 0.0, 2.0));
        scene.set_object_scale(id, Vec3::new(2.0, 0.5, 1.0));
        scene.schedule_rotate(id, 0.0, 90.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
        scene.update(0.5);
        let object = &scene.objects()[&id];
        assert_eq!(
            object.render_data.instance.model,
            object.transform.model().to_cols_array_2d()
        );
        assert_eq!(
            object.render_data.instance.normal_matrix,
            crate::scene::normal_matrix(object.transform.model()).to_cols_array_2d()
        );
        assert_eq!(std::mem::size_of::<InstanceData>(), 144);
    }
    #[test]
    fn render_revision_invalidates_on_commands_and_animation_but_not_idle_frames() {
        let mut scene = SceneManager::default();
        let id = scene.add_cube(Vec3::ZERO);
        let mut previous = scene.render_revision();
        scene.update(1.0);
        assert_eq!(previous, scene.render_revision());
        scene.set_object_position(id, Vec3::ONE);
        assert_ne!(previous, scene.render_revision());
        previous = scene.render_revision();
        scene.set_object_scale(id, Vec3::splat(2.0));
        assert_ne!(previous, scene.render_revision());
        previous = scene.render_revision();
        scene.set_object_rotation(id, Vec3::ONE);
        assert_ne!(previous, scene.render_revision());
        previous = scene.render_revision();
        scene.set_object_transform(id, Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ZERO, Vec3::ONE);
